Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- artifact-design: Design guidance and fundamentals for Artifacts.\n- artifact-capabilities: Runtime capabilities a published Artifact page can be granted — behavior static HTML cannot provide on its own, such as the page reading live or connected data, keeping state shared across viewers, or updating and republishing itself. Serves this user's live capability roster and the typed call definitions. Load it whenever the user asks for an artifact needing any such runtime behavior.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. {"parentUuid":"b70fa1d8-91f0-4a99-bf44-a9affc1c8971","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9rKLdsRXLYN6Yh6ZPGQ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Let me read the core files in parallel, starting with the agent base, governor core, and spine."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2171,"cache_read_input_tokens":9608,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2171,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9rKHoxMAxsuG8uNVUzK","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"8ac6950b-836c-4725-92ab-2d6ecc403242","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:01.781Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"8ac6950b-836c-4725-92ab-2d6ecc403242","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9rKLdsRXLYN6Yh6ZPGQ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01BTW6H91C9SC1M1rAod7U2D","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/base.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2171,"cache_read_input_tokens":9608,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2171,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9rKHoxMAxsuG8uNVUzK","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"728f11f8-5052-4426-9850-92344affc8ce","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:02.288Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"728f11f8-5052-4426-9850-92344affc8ce","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BTW6H91C9SC1M1rAod7U2D","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The SIGIL agent mesh framework (SIGIL §4/§5). Each agent has a MANDATE and an AUTONOMY\n2\tCEILING (A0–A3); everything it does is a `Proposal` carrying a tier. Proposals at or below the\n3\tauto bar (A1) and at or below the agent's ceiling are AUTO-APPLIED (written to the spine);\n4\tanything higher is QUEUED as a pending record a human must approve — never auto-executed.\n5\t\n6\tThe doctrine is enforced STRUCTURALLY, not by discipline: an agent literally cannot take an\n7\texternal-effect action, because no agent has a send/deploy/spend code path. ENVOY, in\n8\tparticular, drafts only — its ceiling is A2 and there is no method that transmits. Agent output\n9\tis provenance-linked spine records, so a brief or a draft is auditable back to what produced it.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n13\tfrom enum import IntEnum\n14\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n17\t\n18\tAGENTS_SOURCE = \"agent\"\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tclass Tier(IntEnum):\n22\t    \"\"\"Mirror of the WARDEN tiers (SIGIL §5), ordered so `<=` compares autonomy.\"\"\"\n23\t    A0 = 0   # observe / answer\n24\t    A1 = 1   # reversible internal act (write a report/brief/event) — the AUTO bar\n25\t    A2 = 2   # external-visible / semi-reversible (send, calendar write) — QUEUED\n26\t    A3 = 3   # destructive / financial / security — explicit, never auto\n27\t\n28\t    def label(self) -> str:\n29\t        return self.name\n30\t\n31\t\n32\tAUTO_BAR = Tier.A1  # A0/A1 auto-apply; A2/A3 queue for human approval\n33\t\n34\t\n35\t@dataclass\n36\tclass Proposal:\n37\t    \"\"\"One thing an agent wants to do: emit a record (auto) or take a gated action (queued).\"\"\"\n38\t    kind: str                       # spine record kind (event/finding/brief/interaction/draft/...)\n39\t    payload: dict\n40\t    tier: Tier = Tier.A1\n41\t    parent_id: Optional[int] = None\n42\t    supersedes_id: Optional[int] = None   # this record supersedes an earlier one (e.g. a resolution)\n43\t\n44\t\n45\t@dataclass\n46\tclass AgentResult:\n47\t    agent: str\n48\t    applied: List[int] = field(default_factory=list)     # seqs written (auto)\n49\t    queued: List[dict] = field(default_factory=list)      # proposals held for approval\n50\t    notes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tclass Agent:\n54\t    \"\"\"Base agent. Subclasses implement `run()`; the base routes every Proposal through the GOVERNOR\n55\t    (SIGIL §5: kill switch, budgets, promotion — Phase 6) and records the outcome on the spine.\n56\t    Backward compatible: with no kill/cap/promotion the governor reproduces the original gate.\"\"\"\n57\t    name: str = \"AGENT\"\n58\t    mandate: str = \"\"\n59\t    ceiling: Tier = Tier.A1\n60\t\n61\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, governor=None):\n62\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n63\t        if governor is None:\n64\t            from ..governor import Governor\n65\t            governor = Governor(self.store)\n66\t        self.governor = governor\n67\t\n68\t    def run(self) -> AgentResult:\n69\t        raise NotImplementedError\n70\t\n71\t    def _dispatch(self, proposals: List[Proposal]) -> AgentResult:\n72\t        from ..governor import Outcome\n73\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n74\t        for p in proposals:\n75\t            effective = max(p.tier, Tier.A0)\n76\t            # promotion scope = the RECORD KIND (the real action written), not a self-asserted label:\n77\t            # a promotion is \"this agent may auto-approve A2 records of this kind\" (red-pen RP-3).\n78\t            decision = self.governor.decide(agent=self.name, tier=effective,\n79\t                                            ceiling=self.ceiling, scope=p.kind)\n80\t            common = {**p.payload, \"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n81\t                      \"governor\": decision.reason}\n82\t            if decision.outcome == Outcome.AUTO:\n83\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n84\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"auto\"},\n85\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n86\t                res.applied.append(seq)\n87\t            elif decision.outcome == Outcome.QUEUE:\n88\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n89\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"queued\", \"status\": \"awaiting-approval\"},\n90\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n91\t                res.queued.append({\"seq\": seq, \"kind\": p.kind, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n92\t                                   \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\") or p.payload.get(\"to\")})\n93\t            else:  # DENY — kill switch or budget; record the refusal, take no action\n94\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n95\t                                  payload={\"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n96\t                                           \"decision\": \"denied\", \"governor\": decision.reason,\n97\t                                           \"of_kind\": p.kind, \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\")},\n98\t                                  parent_id=p.parent_id)\n99\t                res.notes.append(f\"DENIED {p.kind} ({effective.label()}): {decision.reason}\")\n100\t        return res\n101\t"}]},"uuid":"8b8f7c29-9a10-4292-b3c0-17cb9dd06d18","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:02.309Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"728f11f8-5052-4426-9850-92344affc8ce","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"8b8f7c29-9a10-4292-b3c0-17cb9dd06d18","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9rKLdsRXLYN6Yh6ZPGQ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013asSrdJegdpnEhxhKUQjRv","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/governor/core.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2171,"cache_read_input_tokens":9608,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2171,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9rKHoxMAxsuG8uNVUzK","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"97b0eb76-aeef-4181-b2a9-cde12cbafc19","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:02.798Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"97b0eb76-aeef-4181-b2a9-cde12cbafc19","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013asSrdJegdpnEhxhKUQjRv","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The Governor (SIGIL §5) — the single decision point the agent dispatch gate consults for EVERY\n2\tproposal. It composes the kill switch, budgets, and promotion policy into one fail-closed verdict:\n3\tAUTO (write it), QUEUE (hold for human approval), or DENY (refuse + log). The order matters and is\n4\tdeliberately conservative: observe is always allowed; a kill halts everything above observe; budget\n5\tis checked before any auto-grant; A2 auto-approves only under an explicit promotion; A3 never autos.\n6\t\n7\tKill/promotion are read FRESH per decision (a mid-run kill halts the rest of the run). Backward\n8\tcompatible: with no kill, no cap, and no promotion, A0/A1≤ceiling → AUTO and A2/A3 → QUEUE, exactly\n9\tas the pre-Phase-6 gate behaved.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n13\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n14\tfrom enum import Enum\n15\tfrom typing import Optional\n16\t\n17\tfrom .budget import BudgetCaps, BudgetLedger\n18\tfrom .killswitch import KillSwitch\n19\tfrom .promotion import PromotionPolicy\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tclass Outcome(Enum):\n23\t    AUTO = \"auto\"\n24\t    QUEUE = \"queue\"\n25\t    DENY = \"deny\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n29\tclass Decision:\n30\t    outcome: Outcome\n31\t    reason: str\n32\t\n33\t\n34\tdef _utc_day() -> str:\n35\t    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).date().isoformat()   # runtime bucketing only (never digested)\n36\t\n37\t\n38\tdef _load_caps() -> BudgetCaps:\n39\t    \"\"\"Optional per-agent caps from ~/.sigil/budgets.json ({daily_actions, daily_interrupts}).\n40\t    Absent/malformed → uncapped (budgets opt-in; default behavior unchanged).\"\"\"\n41\t    try:\n42\t        import json\n43\t\n44\t        from ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n45\t        f = SIGIL_HOME / \"budgets.json\"\n46\t        if not f.exists():\n47\t            return BudgetCaps()\n48\t        d = json.loads(f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n49\t        return BudgetCaps(daily_actions=d.get(\"daily_actions\"), daily_interrupts=d.get(\"daily_interrupts\"))\n50\t    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):\n51\t        return BudgetCaps()\n52\t\n53\t\n54\tclass Governor:\n55\t    def __init__(self, store, *, caps: Optional[BudgetCaps] = None, day_iso: Optional[str] = None,\n56\t                 owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n57\t        from .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n58\t        self.store = store\n59\t        ok = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n60\t        tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n61\t        self.kill = KillSwitch(store, owner_key=ok, trusted_pubkey=tp)\n62\t        self.promo = PromotionPolicy(store, owner_key=ok, trusted_pubkey=tp)\n63\t        self.budget = BudgetLedger(store, caps if caps is not None else _load_caps())\n64\t        self._day = day_iso\n65\t\n66\t    def decide(self, *, agent: str, tier, ceiling, scope: Optional[str] = None) -> Decision:\n67\t        from ..agents.base import AUTO_BAR, Tier\n68\t        scope = scope or \"*\"\n69\t\n70\t        # A0 observe/read is ALWAYS allowed — even under a kill switch (perception + memory stay alive).\n71\t        if tier <= Tier.A0:\n72\t            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, \"observe (A0)\")\n73\t\n74\t        # KILL SWITCH: the mesh is halted; nothing above observe may act.\n75\t        if self.kill.is_engaged():\n76\t            return Decision(Outcome.DENY, \"kill switch engaged — agent mesh halted (A0 observe only)\")\n77\t\n78\t        # BUDGET: fail-closed — at cap, deny.\n79\t        over, why = self.budget.over_budget(agent, self._day or _utc_day())\n80\t        if over:\n81\t            return Decision(Outcome.DENY, why)\n82\t\n83\t        # Within the auto bar (A0/A1) and the agent's ceiling → auto, as always.\n84\t        if tier <= AUTO_BAR and tier <= ceiling:\n85\t            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, f\"within auto bar ({tier.label()})\")\n86\t\n87\t        # A2 may auto-approve ONLY under an explicit per-scope promotion (never for ENVOY).\n88\t        if tier == Tier.A2 and tier <= ceiling and self.promo.is_promoted(agent, scope):\n89\t            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, f\"A2 auto-approved by promotion ({agent}/{scope})\")\n90\t\n91\t        # Everything else (A2 unpromoted, A3, or above ceiling) queues for explicit human approval.\n92\t        return Decision(Outcome.QUEUE, f\"queued for approval ({tier.label()})\")\n93\t"}]},"uuid":"fd0edf00-8bcb-4778-8165-a80577375cf2","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:02.840Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"97b0eb76-aeef-4181-b2a9-cde12cbafc19","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"fd0edf00-8bcb-4778-8165-a80577375cf2","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9rKLdsRXLYN6Yh6ZPGQ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013XLp8u79yedVCUnBowau5V","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/spine/models.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2171,"cache_read_input_tokens":9608,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2171,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9rKHoxMAxsuG8uNVUzK","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"bc516e3b-fed9-4e33-aa47-bae19fa7f99c","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:03.288Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"bc516e3b-fed9-4e33-aa47-bae19fa7f99c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013XLp8u79yedVCUnBowau5V","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The episodic spine record — one immutable event, source of truth (SIGIL §6.1).\"\"\"\n2\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n3\t\n4\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n5\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n6\tfrom typing import Any\n7\t\n8\t# event kinds (superset of the CRUCIBLE blackboard EventKind, extended for SIGIL sources)\n9\tKINDS = frozenset({\n10\t    \"message\", \"tool_call\", \"tool_result\", \"decision\", \"commitment\",\n11\t    \"commit\", \"document\", \"brief\", \"refusal\", \"email\", \"session\",\n12\t    \"entity\", \"contradiction\",  # promoted by the ARCHIVIST consolidation pass (§6.3)\n13\t    \"warden_checkpoint\",        # WARDEN (Phase 1) cross-anchors its action-log head here (anti-rollback)\n14\t    \"event\", \"finding\", \"interaction\", \"draft\",  # the agent mesh (Phase 3, §4): SENTINEL/BASTION/ENVOY\n15\t    \"report\", \"pr\",             # Phase 4: SCHOLAR sourced research reports, ARTIFICER PR proposals\n16\t})\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tdef now_iso() -> str:\n20\t    \"\"\"Informational wallclock timestamp. NOT part of the content digest (replay-stable).\"\"\"\n21\t    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n22\t\n23\t\n24\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n25\tclass SpineRecord:\n26\t    \"\"\"One line of the spine. `cert_digest`/`prev_hash`/`entry_hash` are the chain fields;\n27\t    everything above `ts` is the digested content (ts is informational, excluded from the digest).\"\"\"\n28\t    seq: int\n29\t    scope: str\n30\t    kind: str\n31\t    source: str            # \"claude-code\" | \"git\" | \"doc\" | ...\n32\t    actor: str             # \"user\" | \"assistant\" | session id | commit author | ...\n33\t    payload: dict[str, Any]\n34\t    parent_id: int | None\n35\t    supersedes_id: int | None\n36\t    ts: str\n37\t    cert_digest: str\n38\t    prev_hash: str\n39\t    entry_hash: str\n40\t\n41\t    @classmethod\n42\t    def from_dict(cls, d: dict[str, Any]) -> \"SpineRecord\":\n43\t        return cls(\n44\t            seq=d[\"seq\"], scope=d[\"scope\"], kind=d[\"kind\"], source=d[\"source\"],\n45\t            actor=d[\"actor\"], payload=d.get(\"payload\") or {},\n46\t            parent_id=d.get(\"parent_id\"), supersedes_id=d.get(\"supersedes_id\"),\n47\t            ts=d.get(\"ts\", \"\"), cert_digest=d[\"cert_digest\"],\n48\t            prev_hash=d[\"prev_hash\"], entry_hash=d[\"entry_hash\"],\n49\t        )\n50\t\n51\t    def text(self) -> str:\n52\t        \"\"\"Best-effort human/searchable text of the record (for embedding + display).\"\"\"\n53\t        p = self.payload or {}\n54\t        for key in (\"text\", \"content\", \"message\", \"summary\", \"body\", \"title\"):\n55\t            v = p.get(key)\n56\t            if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip():\n57\t                return v\n58\t        # tool records / structured payloads: compact JSON fallback\n59\t        import json\n60\t        return json.dumps(p, ensure_ascii=False)[:4000]\n61\t"}]},"uuid":"42312de8-bc79-45e8-a03a-e2b0e0434f0b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:03.300Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"bc516e3b-fed9-4e33-aa47-bae19fa7f99c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"42312de8-bc79-45e8-a03a-e2b0e0434f0b","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9rKLdsRXLYN6Yh6ZPGQ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01R2nG46c5AC3XZZsN7uzyFG","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/spine/store.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2171,"cache_read_input_tokens":9608,"output_tokens":403,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2171},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":403,"cache_read_input_tokens":9608,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2171,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2171,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9rKHoxMAxsuG8uNVUzK","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"52cacc17-3097-48da-8ef7-d31fd84bae6c","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:03.520Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"52cacc17-3097-48da-8ef7-d31fd84bae6c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01R2nG46c5AC3XZZsN7uzyFG","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Append-only, hash-chained JSONL spine (SIGIL §6.1, D1).\n2\t\n3\tReuses CRUCIBLE's tamper-evident chain verbatim (`sigil.reuse`): each line carries\n4\t`{seq, prev_hash, entry_hash}` where `entry_hash` links prev+cert_digest+seq. The\n5\t`cert_digest` is over the record's CONTENT only (scope/kind/source/actor/payload/\n6\tparent/supersedes) — NOT the wallclock `ts` — so the chain is replay-stable. Appends\n7\tare O(1): read the last line's entry, `append_entry`, write.\n8\t\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\timport json\n12\timport os\n13\tfrom pathlib import Path\n14\tfrom typing import Any, Iterator\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..config import SCOPE, SPINE_PATH\n17\tfrom ..reuse import ChainEntry, append_entry, build_chain, digest_payload, verify_chain\n18\tfrom .models import SpineRecord, now_iso\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef _last_nonempty_line(path: Path) -> str | None:\n22\t    \"\"\"Read the last non-empty line without loading the file (seek-from-end).\"\"\"\n23\t    with path.open(\"rb\") as f:\n24\t        f.seek(0, os.SEEK_END)\n25\t        end = f.tell()\n26\t        if end == 0:\n27\t            return None\n28\t        buf = b\"\"\n29\t        pos = end\n30\t        while pos > 0:\n31\t            step = min(8192, pos)\n32\t            pos -= step\n33\t            f.seek(pos)\n34\t            buf = f.read(step) + buf\n35\t            lines = [ln for ln in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if ln.strip()]\n36\t            if lines and (pos == 0 or buf.count(b\"\\n\") >= 2):\n37\t                return lines[-1].decode(\"utf-8\")\n38\t        lines = [ln for ln in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if ln.strip()]\n39\t        return lines[-1].decode(\"utf-8\") if lines else None\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tclass SpineStore:\n43\t    def __init__(self, path: Path | str = SPINE_PATH) -> None:\n44\t        self.path = Path(path)\n45\t        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n46\t        self._last: ChainEntry | None = self._read_last_entry()\n47\t\n48\t    # --- write --------------------------------------------------------------------\n49\t    def append(\n50\t        self, *, kind: str, source: str, actor: str, payload: dict[str, Any],\n51\t        parent_id: int | None = None, supersedes_id: int | None = None,\n52\t        ts: str | None = None,\n53\t    ) -> int:\n54\t        content = {\n55\t            \"scope\": SCOPE, \"kind\": kind, \"source\": source, \"actor\": actor,\n56\t            \"payload\": payload, \"parent_id\": parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": supersedes_id,\n57\t        }\n58\t        cert_digest = digest_payload(content)  # wallclock-free\n59\t        entry = append_entry([self._last], cert_digest) if self._last else build_chain([cert_digest])[0]\n60\t        record = {\n61\t            \"seq\": entry.seq, **content, \"ts\": ts or now_iso(),\n62\t            \"cert_digest\": cert_digest, \"prev_hash\": entry.prev_hash, \"entry_hash\": entry.entry_hash,\n63\t        }\n64\t        with self.path.open(\"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n65\t            f.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n66\t        self._last = entry\n67\t        return entry.seq\n68\t\n69\t    # --- read ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n70\t    def iter_records(self, *, since_seq: int = -1) -> Iterator[SpineRecord]:\n71\t        if not self.path.exists():\n72\t            return\n73\t        with self.path.open(encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n74\t            for line in f:\n75\t                line = line.strip()\n76\t                if not line:\n77\t                    continue\n78\t                d = json.loads(line)\n79\t                if d[\"seq\"] > since_seq:\n80\t                    yield SpineRecord.from_dict(d)\n81\t\n82\t    def get(self, seq: int) -> SpineRecord | None:\n83\t        for r in self.iter_records(since_seq=seq - 1):\n84\t            if r.seq == seq:\n85\t                return r\n86\t            if r.seq > seq:\n87\t                break\n88\t        return None\n89\t\n90\t    @property\n91\t    def next_seq(self) -> int:\n92\t        return (self._last.seq + 1) if self._last else 0\n93\t\n94\t    def count(self) -> int:\n95\t        return sum(1 for _ in self.iter_records())\n96\t\n97\t    # --- integrity ----------------------------------------------------------------\n98\t    def entries(self) -> list[ChainEntry]:\n99\t        return [\n100\t            ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash)\n101\t            for r in self.iter_records()\n102\t        ]\n103\t\n104\t    def verify(self) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n105\t        \"\"\"Two-layer integrity: (1) BINDING — each record's payload still hashes to its\n106\t        stored cert_digest (catches silent payload edits); (2) CHAIN — the entries link\n107\t        cleanly (catches delete/reorder/entry tamper). A payload edit fails (1); editing\n108\t        the digest to match cascades an entry_hash/prev_hash break caught by (2).\"\"\"\n109\t        entries: list[ChainEntry] = []\n110\t        for r in self.iter_records():\n111\t            content = {\n112\t                \"scope\": r.scope, \"kind\": r.kind, \"source\": r.source, \"actor\": r.actor,\n113\t                \"payload\": r.payload, \"parent_id\": r.parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": r.supersedes_id,\n114\t            }\n115\t            if digest_payload(content) != r.cert_digest:\n116\t                return False, f\"binding break at seq {r.seq}: payload does not match cert_digest (record tampered)\"\n117\t            entries.append(ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash))\n118\t        return verify_chain(entries)\n119\t\n120\t    def _read_last_entry(self) -> ChainEntry | None:\n121\t        line = _last_nonempty_line(self.path) if self.path.exists() else None\n122\t        if not line:\n123\t            return None\n124\t        d = json.loads(line)\n125\t        return ChainEntry(seq=d[\"seq\"], prev_hash=d[\"prev_hash\"], cert_digest=d[\"cert_digest\"], entry_hash=d[\"entry_hash\"])\n126\t"}]},"uuid":"e8994269-339a-4c02-b01f-bd28bb7eb3b1","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:03.528Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"52cacc17-3097-48da-8ef7-d31fd84bae6c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"8461ca34-425e-480a-9c70-f4897fa9d5a0","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9rKmohCrv8vR2PFK57L","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01YMDGJMs2Swoc2YkF3mcV39","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/artificer.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":7930,"cache_read_input_tokens":11779,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":7930,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9rKktsoDYkrzWRr64P9","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"8331119a-88a6-4010-bf73-79b75c9e640e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:10.186Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"8331119a-88a6-4010-bf73-79b75c9e640e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YMDGJMs2Swoc2YkF3mcV39","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"ARTIFICER (SIGIL §4.4) — engineering: drives headless Claude Code against a repo to own a\n2\tcoding task end to end. Ceiling A2 (a PR ready-for-review queues for approval); `git push` to a\n3\tprotected branch, deploys, and dependency additions are A3 — ARTIFICER NEVER pushes.\n4\t\n5\tCorrectness discipline (the §4.4 focus): it runs the tests BEFORE claiming done — a PR is\n6\tproposed ONLY if the test command passes on the change; a failing change is reported, not\n7\tshipped. The result is a local branch + a plain-language diff summary, QUEUED for the human to\n8\treview and push. The coder is pluggable: `ClaudeCoder` (headless `claude -p`) or a deterministic\n9\ttest double.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport re\n13\timport shutil\n14\timport subprocess\n15\timport tempfile\n16\tfrom pathlib import Path\n17\tfrom typing import List, Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n18\t\n19\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n20\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n21\t\n22\t# commands that \"pass\" trivially — a PR must never claim tests passing on one of these.\n23\t_TRIVIAL_TEST = frozenset({(), (\"true\",), (\":\",), (\"/bin/true\",), (\"/usr/bin/true\",)})\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef _git(repo: str, *args: str, check: bool = True) -> str:\n27\t    proc = subprocess.run([\"git\", \"-C\", repo, *args], capture_output=True, text=True)\n28\t    if check and proc.returncode != 0:\n29\t        raise RuntimeError(f\"git {' '.join(args)} failed: {proc.stderr.strip()}\")\n30\t    return proc.stdout\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef _slug(task: str) -> str:\n34\t    base = re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", task.lower()).strip(\"-\")[:28] or \"task\"\n35\t    return f\"{base}-{sha256_hex(task.encode())[:6]}\"   # unique per task, stable\n36\t\n37\t\n38\t@runtime_checkable\n39\tclass Coder(Protocol):\n40\t    def code(self, task: str, workdir: str) -> str: ...   # perform the change; return a summary\n41\t\n42\t\n43\tclass ClaudeCoder:\n44\t    \"\"\"Headless Claude Code (`claude -p`) run inside the repo — the real ARTIFICER worker.\"\"\"\n45\t    def __init__(self, claude_bin: str = \"/home/kali/.local/bin/claude\", timeout: int = 600):\n46\t        self.claude_bin, self.timeout = claude_bin, timeout\n47\t\n48\t    def code(self, task: str, workdir: str) -> str:\n49\t        prompt = (f\"You are working in the repo at {workdir}. Task: {task}\\n\"\n50\t                  \"Make the minimal change to accomplish it. Do not run git. Do not touch unrelated files.\")\n51\t        # acceptEdits lets the headless coder actually apply Edit/Write in its sandboxed repo (it\n52\t        # cannot prompt for permission in -p mode). ARTIFICER's isolation is the per-task branch;\n53\t        # git push / deploy stay A3 and are never invoked here.\n54\t        try:\n55\t            proc = subprocess.run(\n56\t                [self.claude_bin, \"-p\", prompt, \"--permission-mode\", \"acceptEdits\"],\n57\t                cwd=workdir, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n58\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as e:\n59\t            return f\"(coder error: {e})\"\n60\t        return (proc.stdout or \"\").strip()[:400]\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tclass Artificer(Agent):\n64\t    name = \"ARTIFICER\"\n65\t    mandate = \"own background coding tasks: tests before done, PRs not pushes\"\n66\t    ceiling = Tier.A2\n67\t\n68\t    def run(self, task: str, *, repo: str, test_cmd: Optional[List[str]] = None,\n69\t            coder: Optional[Coder] = None, branch: Optional[str] = None) -> AgentResult:\n70\t        coder = coder or ClaudeCoder()\n71\t        base = _git(repo, \"rev-parse\", \"--abbrev-ref\", \"HEAD\").strip()\n72\t        branch = branch or f\"artificer/{_slug(task)}\"\n73\t\n74\t        # ISOLATION: the coder works in a dedicated git WORKTREE off `base`, so the real working\n75\t        # tree is never touched, no pre-existing/unrelated edits get bundled, and a failure leaves\n76\t        # the main repo exactly as it was. The per-task branch survives for the human to review.\n77\t        _git(repo, \"worktree\", \"prune\", check=False)\n78\t        wt = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"artificer-wt-\")\n79\t        _git(repo, \"worktree\", \"add\", \"-B\", branch, wt, base)\n80\t        try:\n81\t            summary = coder.code(task, wt)\n82\t            changed = bool(_git(wt, \"status\", \"--porcelain\", check=False).strip())\n83\t            diff = _git(wt, \"diff\", \"--stat\", check=False).strip()\n84\t\n85\t            if not changed:\n86\t                res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n87\t                res.notes.append(\"no change produced for the task — nothing to propose.\")\n88\t                return res\n89\t\n90\t            real_test = test_cmd is not None and tuple(test_cmd) not in _TRIVIAL_TEST\n91\t            if not real_test:\n92\t                # NEVER claim \"tests passing\" without a real test command — record UNVERIFIED, withhold PR.\n93\t                res = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"finding\", {\n94\t                    \"summary\": f\"ARTIFICER task '{task}': change made but NO real test command was run — UNVERIFIED, PR withheld\",\n95\t                    \"branch\": branch, \"diffstat\": diff, \"coder\": summary}, Tier.A1)])\n96\t                res.notes.append(\"no real test command → change is UNVERIFIED; PR withheld (correctness discipline)\")\n97\t                return res\n98\t\n99\t            test = subprocess.run(test_cmd, cwd=wt, capture_output=True, text=True)\n100\t            if test.returncode != 0:\n101\t                tail = (test.stdout + test.stderr).strip().splitlines()\n102\t                res = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"finding\", {\n103\t                    \"summary\": f\"ARTIFICER task '{task}': change made but tests FAIL — PR withheld\",\n104\t                    \"branch\": branch, \"test_tail\": tail[-3:] if tail else [], \"diffstat\": diff}, Tier.A1)])\n105\t                res.notes.append(\"tests failed after the change — PR withheld (correctness discipline)\")\n106\t                return res\n107\t\n108\t            # tests genuinely pass → commit on the branch (in the worktree), propose a PR (A2, QUEUED).\n109\t            _git(wt, \"add\", \"-A\")\n110\t            _git(wt, \"commit\", \"-m\", f\"ARTIFICER: {task}\", check=False)\n111\t            res = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"pr\", {\n112\t                \"subject\": task, \"branch\": branch, \"base\": base, \"diffstat\": diff,\n113\t                \"summary\": summary, \"tests\": f\"passing ({' '.join(test_cmd)})\",\n114\t                \"note\": \"ready-for-review; ARTIFICER does NOT push — a human reviews and pushes\"}, Tier.A2)])\n115\t            res.notes.append(f\"task done, tests PASS → PR proposed on branch '{branch}' (A2, awaiting approval)\")\n116\t            return res\n117\t        finally:\n118\t            _git(repo, \"worktree\", \"remove\", \"--force\", wt, check=False)\n119\t            shutil.rmtree(wt, ignore_errors=True)\n120\t"}]},"uuid":"9c0de984-f05a-44e0-a27e-ca25f4f00f07","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:10.235Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8331119a-88a6-4010-bf73-79b75c9e640e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"c737fcbd-e49d-4802-a177-6c0442d31e5c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XZuSi79S6WzzGxeRoxbZmq","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"BASTION (SIGIL §4.8) — defensive security posture over the owner's OWN infrastructure ONLY.\n2\tCeiling A1 (writes findings; remediation/patching is A3 and never taken here). Checks: TLS cert\n3\texpiry, dependency CVE exposure, and uptime — all OBSERVATIONAL. There is NO exploit, no port\n4\tsweep, no third-party target: BASTION iterates an allowlisted asset INVENTORY, and any target not\n5\tin that allowlist is REFUSED and logged (`refusal` record). This makes \"own systems only\" a\n6\tstructural property, not a promise (SIGIL §4.8 doctrine; enforced, not documented).\n7\t\n8\tGROUNDING (serve-the-quote, reused from SCHOLAR/consolidation): every finding carries the verbatim\n9\tobserved fact it stands on — the real cert `notAfter`, the exact manifest line, the probe status —\n10\tnever a model's guess. A dependency is flagged ONLY when its parsed version PROVABLY falls in an\n11\tadvisory's affected range; an unparseable version is a non-assessment, never a fabricated CVE.\"\"\"\n12\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n13\t\n14\timport re\n15\timport socket\n16\timport ssl\n17\timport urllib.request\n18\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n19\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n20\tfrom pathlib import Path\n21\tfrom typing import List, Optional, Protocol, Tuple, runtime_checkable\n22\t\n23\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n24\t\n25\t_PIN = re.compile(r\"^([A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]*)\\s*==\\s*([0-9][0-9A-Za-z.\\-]*)\")\n26\t\n27\t\n28\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n29\tclass Asset:\n30\t    \"\"\"One allowlisted own-infra asset. `kind` ∈ {tls, deps, uptime}; `ref` is host:port / manifest\n31\t    path / url. Membership in the inventory IS the authorization to observe it.\"\"\"\n32\t    name: str\n33\t    kind: str\n34\t    ref: str\n35\t    meta: Optional[dict] = None\n36\t\n37\t\n38\t# --- version reasoning (strict, fail-closed to NON-assessment — never a fabricated vuln) ----------\n39\tdef _ver_tuple(v: Optional[str]) -> Optional[tuple]:\n40\t    \"\"\"A purely numeric dotted version → tuple; anything else (rc/beta/git/empty) → None (can't\n41\t    assess). Being strict here is what keeps CVE matching near-zero-false-positive.\"\"\"\n42\t    if not v:\n43\t        return None\n44\t    parts = str(v).strip().lstrip(\"vV\").split(\".\")\n45\t    out = []\n46\t    for p in parts:\n47\t        # ASCII decimals ONLY — str.isdigit() also accepts superscripts/other-script digits that\n48\t        # int() rejects or reinterprets, so guard on isascii() too (truly fail-closed, never a crash).\n49\t        if not (p.isascii() and p.isdigit()):\n50\t            return None\n51\t        out.append(int(p))\n52\t    return tuple(out) if out else None\n53\t\n54\t\n55\tdef _cmp(a: tuple, b: tuple) -> int:\n56\t    n = max(len(a), len(b))\n57\t    a = a + (0,) * (n - len(a))\n58\t    b = b + (0,) * (n - len(b))\n59\t    return (a > b) - (a < b)\n60\t\n61\t\n62\tdef _affected(version: str, introduced: Optional[str], fixed: Optional[str]) -> Optional[bool]:\n63\t    \"\"\"True iff introduced <= version < fixed. None if it cannot be proven either way (unparseable\n64\t    version/bound) — the caller treats None as 'no finding' (honest non-assessment).\"\"\"\n65\t    vt = _ver_tuple(version)\n66\t    if vt is None:\n67\t        return None\n68\t    it = _ver_tuple(introduced) if introduced else (0,)\n69\t    if it is None:\n70\t        return None\n71\t    if fixed:\n72\t        ft = _ver_tuple(fixed)\n73\t        if ft is None:\n74\t            return None\n75\t        if _cmp(vt, ft) >= 0:\n76\t            return False\n77\t    return _cmp(vt, it) >= 0\n78\t\n79\t\n80\t# --- capture seams (real by default, injectable doubles for offline tests) ------------------------\n81\t@runtime_checkable\n82\tclass CertSource(Protocol):\n83\t    def pem(self, ref: str) -> Optional[str]: ...    # ref=\"host:port\" → leaf cert PEM, or None\n84\t\n85\t\n86\t@runtime_checkable\n87\tclass UptimeSource(Protocol):\n88\t    def probe(self, ref: str) -> Tuple[bool, int]: ...   # (up, status_code)\n89\t\n90\t\n91\tclass SocketCertSource:\n92\t    \"\"\"Fetch the leaf TLS cert from an OWN host. Only ever called for allowlisted refs (BASTION\n93\t    guards before calling). Returns None on any error (no reachable cert = no finding).\"\"\"\n94\t    def __init__(self, timeout: int = 8):\n95\t        self.timeout = timeout\n96\t\n97\t    def pem(self, ref: str) -> Optional[str]:\n98\t        host, _, port = ref.partition(\":\")\n99\t        try:\n100\t            return ssl.get_server_certificate((host, int(port or 443)), timeout=self.timeout)\n101\t        except (OSError, ssl.SSLError, ValueError):\n102\t            return None\n103\t\n104\t\n105\tclass _NoRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):\n106\t    \"\"\"Refuse to follow 3xx. A redirect target is chosen by the (possibly compromised) allowlisted\n107\t    server and is NOT itself allowlisted — following it would let BASTION's probe reach a host outside\n108\t    the own-infra scope, silently and un-audited (red-pen RP-BASTION-01). The 3xx is the status.\"\"\"\n109\t    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):\n110\t        return None\n111\t\n112\t\n113\tclass UrllibUptimeSource:\n114\t    def __init__(self, timeout: int = 8):\n115\t        self.timeout = timeout\n116\t        self._opener = urllib.request.build_opener(_NoRedirect)   # never auto-follows a redirect\n117\t\n118\t    def probe(self, ref: str) -> Tuple[bool, int]:\n119\t        try:\n120\t            req = urllib.request.Request(ref, method=\"HEAD\",\n121\t                                         headers={\"User-Agent\": \"SIGIL-BASTION/1.0 (own-infra uptime)\"})\n122\t            with self._opener.open(req, timeout=self.timeout) as r:\n123\t                return (200 <= r.status < 500, r.status)\n124\t        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:       # a 5xx is 'down'; a 3xx (not followed) is 'up' + its code\n125\t            return (e.code < 500, e.code)\n126\t        except (OSError, ValueError):\n127\t            return (False, 0)\n128\t\n129\t\n130\tclass Bastion(Agent):\n131\t    name = \"BASTION\"\n132\t    mandate = \"defensive posture over OWN infrastructure only; observe, never remediate\"\n133\t    ceiling = Tier.A1\n134\t\n135\t    def __init__(self, store=None, *, inventory: Optional[List[Asset]] = None,\n136\t                 cve_feed: Optional[List[dict]] = None,\n137\t                 cert_source: Optional[CertSource] = None,\n138\t                 uptime_source: Optional[UptimeSource] = None):\n139\t        super().__init__(store)\n140\t        self.inventory = list(inventory or [])\n141\t        self._allow = {a.ref for a in self.inventory}          # THE own-infra allowlist\n142\t        self.cve_feed = list(cve_feed or [])\n143\t        self.cert_source = cert_source or SocketCertSource()\n144\t        self.uptime_source = uptime_source or UrllibUptimeSource()\n145\t\n146\t    # --- scope doctrine (structural own-infra-only) -----------------------------------------------\n147\t    def _refuse(self, ref: str, reason: str) -> int:\n148\t        return self.store.append(\n149\t            kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n150\t            payload={\"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"requested\": ref,\n151\t                     \"reason\": reason,\n152\t                     \"doctrine\": \"BASTION observes only the allowlisted own-infra inventory (SIGIL §4.8); \"\n153\t                                 \"no third-party scanning, no exploit tooling.\"})\n154\t\n155\t    def probe_target(self, ref: str, kind: str, *, now_iso: Optional[str] = None) -> AgentResult:\n156\t        \"\"\"Guarded ad-hoc entry. A ref outside the own-infra allowlist is REFUSED and logged — it is\n157\t        never scanned. This is the doctrine's structural teeth.\"\"\"\n158\t        if ref not in self._allow:\n159\t            self._refuse(ref, \"ad-hoc target is not in the own-infra allowlist\")\n160\t            res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n161\t            res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED {ref}: outside own-infra scope (logged, not scanned)\")\n162\t            return res\n163\t        return self._dispatch(self._assess(Asset(name=ref, kind=kind, ref=ref), now_iso=now_iso))\n164\t\n165\t    def _assess(self, asset: Asset, *, now_iso: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Proposal]:\n166\t        if asset.ref not in self._allow:            # defense in depth — never scan an unlisted ref\n167\t            self._refuse(asset.ref, \"asset not in the own-infra allowlist\")\n168\t            return []\n169\t        if asset.kind == \"tls\":\n170\t            return self._cert(asset, now_iso)\n171\t        if asset.kind == \"deps\":\n172\t            return self._deps(asset)\n173\t        if asset.kind == \"uptime\":\n174\t            return self._uptime(asset)\n175\t        return []\n176\t\n177\t    # --- scanners (observational; each finding carries its verbatim ground truth) -----------------\n178\t    def _cert(self, asset: Asset, now_iso: Optional[str]) -> List[Proposal]:\n179\t        pem = self.cert_source.pem(asset.ref)\n180\t        if not pem:\n181\t            return []\n182\t        try:\n183\t            from cryptography import x509\n184\t            cert = x509.load_pem_x509_certificate(pem.encode())\n185\t            not_after = getattr(cert, \"not_valid_after_utc\", None) or \\\n186\t                cert.not_valid_after.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n187\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — an unparseable cert is no finding, not a crash\n188\t            return []\n189\t        now = datetime.fromisoformat(now_iso) if now_iso else datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n190\t        if now.tzinfo is None:\n191\t            now = now.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n192\t        days = (not_after - now).days\n193\t        threshold = (asset.meta or {}).get(\"expiry_days\", 30)\n194\t        if days > threshold:\n195\t            return []\n196\t        sev = \"critical\" if days <= 0 else (\"high\" if days <= 7 else \"medium\")\n197\t        state = \"EXPIRED\" if days < 0 else f\"expires in {days}d\"\n198\t        return [Proposal(\"finding\", {\n199\t            \"check\": \"tls-cert-expiry\", \"asset\": asset.name, \"ref\": asset.ref, \"severity\": sev,\n200\t            \"days_to_expiry\": days,\n201\t            \"quote\": f\"notAfter={not_after.isoformat()}\",         # verbatim observed ground truth\n202\t            \"summary\": f\"TLS cert for {asset.name} {state} ({not_after.date()})\",\n203\t            \"assessed_at\": now.isoformat()}, Tier.A1)]\n204\t\n205\t    def _deps(self, asset: Asset) -> List[Proposal]:\n206\t        try:\n207\t            lines = Path(asset.ref).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"ignore\").splitlines()\n208\t        except OSError:\n209\t            return []\n210\t        props: List[Proposal] = []\n211\t        for raw in lines:\n212\t            m = _PIN.match(raw.strip())\n213\t            if not m:\n214\t                continue\n215\t            pkg, ver = m.group(1).lower(), m.group(2)\n216\t            for adv in self.cve_feed:\n217\t                if str(adv.get(\"package\", \"\")).lower() != pkg:\n218\t                    continue\n219\t                if _affected(ver, adv.get(\"introduced\"), adv.get(\"fixed\")) is not True:\n220\t                    continue                         # only a PROVEN match fires (None/False → skip)\n221\t                props.append(Proposal(\"finding\", {\n222\t                    \"check\": \"dependency-cve\", \"asset\": asset.name, \"ref\": asset.ref,\n223\t                    \"severity\": str(adv.get(\"severity\", \"medium\")), \"cve\": adv.get(\"id\"),\n224\t                    \"package\": pkg, \"version\": ver,\n225\t                    \"quote\": raw.strip(),            # verbatim manifest line — the ground truth\n226\t                    \"advisory\": f\"{adv.get('id')}: affected {adv.get('introduced') or '0'} \"\n227\t                                f\"..< {adv.get('fixed') or '∞'}\",\n228\t                    \"summary\": f\"{pkg} {ver} is affected by {adv.get('id')} \"\n229\t                               f\"({str(adv.get('summary', ''))[:60]})\",\n230\t                    \"fixed_in\": adv.get(\"fixed\")}, Tier.A1))\n231\t        return props\n232\t\n233\t    def _uptime(self, asset: Asset) -> List[Proposal]:\n234\t        up, status = self.uptime_source.probe(asset.ref)\n235\t        if up:\n236\t            return []\n237\t        return [Proposal(\"finding\", {\n238\t            \"check\": \"uptime\", \"asset\": asset.name, \"ref\": asset.ref, \"severity\": \"high\",\n239\t            \"quote\": f\"HEAD {asset.ref} → status={status}\",\n240\t            \"summary\": f\"{asset.name} appears DOWN (probe status {status})\"}, Tier.A1)]\n241\t\n242\t    @staticmethod\n243\t    def _key(payload: dict) -> tuple:\n244\t        \"\"\"A finding's IDENTITY: (asset, check, cve). The `cve` discriminator keeps N concurrent CVEs\n245\t        on one manifest distinct (RP-2) and lets a re-run resolve exactly the right prior finding.\"\"\"\n246\t        return (payload.get(\"asset\"), payload.get(\"check\"), payload.get(\"cve\"))\n247\t\n248\t    def _resolutions(self, fired_keys: set) -> List[Proposal]:\n249\t        \"\"\"For any (asset,check,cve) we flagged before that this run did NOT re-flag — and whose asset\n250\t        we actually re-assessed — emit a `resolved` supersession, so the brief stops presenting a fixed\n251\t        problem as current (RP-1/RP-5). A resolution supersedes the stale finding (latest wins).\"\"\"\n252\t        assessed = {a.name for a in self.inventory}\n253\t        latest: dict = {}\n254\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n255\t            if r.kind == \"finding\" and r.actor == self.name:\n256\t                latest[self._key(r.payload)] = r        # iter is seq-ascending → last seen = newest\n257\t        props: List[Proposal] = []\n258\t        for key, r in latest.items():\n259\t            if r.payload.get(\"resolved\"):\n260\t                continue                                # already resolved — don't churn\n261\t            asset_name, check, cve = key\n262\t            if asset_name in assessed and key not in fired_keys:\n263\t                props.append(Proposal(\"finding\", {\n264\t                    \"check\": check, \"asset\": asset_name, \"cve\": cve, \"resolved\": True,\n265\t                    \"severity\": \"info\", \"resolves_seq\": r.seq,\n266\t                    \"summary\": f\"{asset_name} {check}{(' ' + str(cve)) if cve else ''} resolved — re-assessed clean\",\n267\t                    \"quote\": \"resolved: prior finding no longer reproduces\"},\n268\t                    Tier.A1, supersedes_id=r.seq))\n269\t        return props\n270\t\n271\t    def run(self, *, now_iso: Optional[str] = None) -> AgentResult:\n272\t        proposals: List[Proposal] = []\n273\t        for asset in self.inventory:\n274\t            proposals.extend(self._assess(asset, now_iso=now_iso))\n275\t        fired = {self._key(p.payload) for p in proposals}\n276\t        resolutions = self._resolutions(fired)\n277\t        res = self._dispatch(proposals + resolutions)\n278\t        res.notes.append(f\"assessed {len(self.inventory)} own-infra asset(s) → {len(proposals)} finding(s)\"\n279\t                         + (f\", {len(resolutions)} resolved\" if resolutions else \"\"))\n280\t        return res\n281\t"}]},"uuid":"3d4383b1-c3ed-4a29-b80d-ad02dab1e47d","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:10.722Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c737fcbd-e49d-4802-a177-6c0442d31e5c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"4a68e24b-7dba-4b4d-a925-21749e6cba2c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01V5iWQMbh2suLLmo2ggZozr","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Source reading for SCHOLAR — a URL (web fetch, HTML stripped) or a local file/doc path → text.\n2\tKept small + dependency-free (urllib + a crude tag strip); a richer extractor is a later add.\n3\t\n4\tSSRF GATE (Phase 6, closes the carried debt + red-pen SSRF-REBIND-TOCTOU): the host is resolved\n5\tONCE; every resolved address must be public (not private/loopback/link-local/reserved/multicast/\n6\tunspecified — IPv4 and IPv6, incl. IPv4-mapped IPv6); and the connection is then PINNED to that exact\n7\tvetted IP, so a rebinding DNS that flips to an internal address between the check and the fetch cannot\n8\tsteer SCHOLAR onto `169.254.169.254`/`localhost`/an internal service. Redirects are not followed. A\n9\trefused/unreadable source is simply empty evidence, never a fabricated one.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport http.client\n13\timport ipaddress\n14\timport re\n15\timport socket\n16\timport urllib.request\n17\tfrom pathlib import Path\n18\tfrom typing import Optional\n19\tfrom urllib.parse import urlsplit\n20\t\n21\t_TAG = re.compile(r\"<[^>]+>\")\n22\t_WS = re.compile(r\"\\s+\")\n23\t_SCRIPT = re.compile(r\"<(script|style)[^>]*>.*?</\\1>\", re.S | re.I)\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef _strip_html(html: str) -> str:\n27\t    html = _SCRIPT.sub(\" \", html)\n28\t    return _WS.sub(\" \", _TAG.sub(\" \", html)).strip()\n29\t\n30\t\n31\tdef _vetted_ip(host: str) -> Optional[str]:\n32\t    \"\"\"Resolve `host`; return ONE address to pin iff EVERY resolved address is public unicast, else\n33\t    None. Fail-closed: an unresolvable host, or any private/loopback/link-local/reserved/multicast/\n34\t    unspecified/IPv4-mapped-internal address, yields None.\"\"\"\n35\t    if not host:\n36\t        return None\n37\t    try:\n38\t        infos = socket.getaddrinfo(host, None)\n39\t    except (socket.gaierror, UnicodeError, OSError):\n40\t        return None\n41\t    if not infos:\n42\t        return None\n43\t    chosen = None\n44\t    for family, _t, _p, _c, sockaddr in infos:\n45\t        try:\n46\t            ip = ipaddress.ip_address(sockaddr[0])\n47\t        except ValueError:\n48\t            return None\n49\t        # unwrap IPv4-mapped/compat IPv6 (::ffff:127.0.0.1) so an internal v4 can't hide as v6\n50\t        if isinstance(ip, ipaddress.IPv6Address) and ip.ipv4_mapped is not None:\n51\t            ip = ip.ipv4_mapped\n52\t        if (ip.is_private or ip.is_loopback or ip.is_link_local or ip.is_reserved\n53\t                or ip.is_multicast or ip.is_unspecified):\n54\t            return None\n55\t        if chosen is None:\n56\t            chosen = sockaddr[0]\n57\t    return chosen\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef is_public_host(host: str) -> bool:\n61\t    return _vetted_ip(host) is not None\n62\t\n63\t\n64\tclass _PinnedHTTPConnection(http.client.HTTPConnection):\n65\t    pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None\n66\t\n67\t    def connect(self):\n68\t        self.sock = socket.create_connection((self.pinned_ip or self.host, self.port),\n69\t                                              self.timeout, self.source_address)\n70\t        if getattr(self, \"_tunnel_host\", None):\n71\t            self._tunnel()\n72\t\n73\t\n74\tclass _PinnedHTTPSConnection(http.client.HTTPSConnection):\n75\t    pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None\n76\t\n77\t    def connect(self):\n78\t        sock = socket.create_connection((self.pinned_ip or self.host, self.port),\n79\t                                        self.timeout, self.source_address)\n80\t        if getattr(self, \"_tunnel_host\", None):\n81\t            self.sock = sock\n82\t            self._tunnel()\n83\t            sock = self.sock\n84\t        # SNI + certificate validation still use the ORIGINAL hostname, not the pinned IP.\n85\t        self.sock = self._context.wrap_socket(sock, server_hostname=self.host)\n86\t\n87\t\n88\tclass _PinnedHTTPHandler(urllib.request.HTTPHandler):\n89\t    def __init__(self, ip):\n90\t        super().__init__()\n91\t        self._ip = ip\n92\t\n93\t    def http_open(self, req):\n94\t        ip = self._ip\n95\t\n96\t        def factory(host, **kw):\n97\t            c = _PinnedHTTPConnection(host, **kw)\n98\t            c.pinned_ip = ip\n99\t            return c\n100\t        return self.do_open(factory, req)\n101\t\n102\t\n103\tclass _PinnedHTTPSHandler(urllib.request.HTTPSHandler):\n104\t    def __init__(self, ip):\n105\t        super().__init__()\n106\t        self._ip = ip\n107\t\n108\t    def https_open(self, req):\n109\t        ip = self._ip\n110\t\n111\t        def factory(host, **kw):\n112\t            c = _PinnedHTTPSConnection(host, **kw)\n113\t            c.pinned_ip = ip\n114\t            return c\n115\t        return self.do_open(factory, req, context=self._context)\n116\t\n117\t\n118\tclass _NoRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):\n119\t    \"\"\"Refuse to follow 3xx — a public URL must not redirect SCHOLAR onto an internal host.\"\"\"\n120\t    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):\n121\t        return None\n122\t\n123\t\n124\tdef read_source(ref: str, *, timeout: int = 20, max_chars: int = 20000) -> str:\n125\t    \"\"\"Return the text of a source. `http(s)://…` is SSRF-gated (resolve-once, verify-all-public,\n126\t    pin the IP, no redirects) then fetched + HTML-stripped; anything else is a local path. Never\n127\t    raises — returns '' on failure or refusal.\"\"\"\n128\t    try:\n129\t        if ref.startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\")):\n130\t            host = urlsplit(ref).hostname or \"\"\n131\t            ip = _vetted_ip(host)\n132\t            if ip is None:\n133\t                return \"\"       # SSRF gate: non-public / unresolvable → no request leaves the machine\n134\t            opener = urllib.request.build_opener(_PinnedHTTPHandler(ip), _PinnedHTTPSHandler(ip), _NoRedirect)\n135\t            req = urllib.request.Request(ref, headers={\"User-Agent\": \"SIGIL-SCHOLAR/1.0 (authorized owner research)\"})\n136\t            with opener.open(req, timeout=timeout) as r:\n137\t                raw = r.read().decode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")\n138\t            return _strip_html(raw)[:max_chars]\n139\t        return Path(ref).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"ignore\")[:max_chars]\n140\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — an unreadable source is simply empty evidence\n141\t        return \"\"\n142\t"}]},"uuid":"a09b8414-edb5-40b4-8a8a-0864745312cb","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:10.738Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4a68e24b-7dba-4b4d-a925-21749e6cba2c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"ce906f9f-647d-48d9-88ad-7f175a9405f0","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014pitmJJZQjVCx4yJr9Z9NH","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The kill switch (SIGIL §5) — a single latch that halts the agent mesh while leaving perception and\n2\tmemory-read (A0 observe) alive. State is append-only on the spine.\n3\t\n4\tASYMMETRIC AUTHENTICATION (Phase 6 red-pen RP-2): halting is always the safe direction, so ANY\n5\tengage event halts (a nuisance forged-engage is at worst a fail-safe DoS). UN-halting is the\n6\tdangerous direction, so a RELEASE is honored ONLY if it is signed by the owner key and verifies\n7\tagainst the trusted pubkey — a forged release can never revive a halted mesh. Read fresh per decision\n8\tso a release (or engage) takes effect immediately.\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\tfrom typing import Optional\n12\t\n13\tfrom .authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n14\tfrom .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n15\t\n16\tSIGNAL = \"governor.killswitch\"\n17\t_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\")\n18\t\n19\t\n20\tclass KillSwitch:\n21\t    def __init__(self, store, *, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n22\t        self.store = store\n23\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n24\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n25\t\n26\t    def engage(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n27\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"engaged\"}\n28\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n29\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n30\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n31\t\n32\t    def release(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n33\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"released\"}\n34\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n35\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n36\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n37\t\n38\t    def is_engaged(self) -> bool:\n39\t        engaged = False\n40\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n41\t            p = r.payload\n42\t            if p.get(\"signal\") != SIGNAL:\n43\t                continue\n44\t            state = p.get(\"state\")\n45\t            if state == \"engaged\":\n46\t                engaged = True                      # honor ANY engage — halting is fail-safe\n47\t            elif state == \"released\" and verify_signed(p, _CORE, self.trusted_pubkey):\n48\t                engaged = False                     # only an OWNER-SIGNED release un-halts (fail-closed)\n49\t        return engaged\n50\t"}]},"uuid":"12c38833-9eb8-4f0e-a9f0-449e258e5b11","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:17.744Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ce906f9f-647d-48d9-88ad-7f175a9405f0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"12c38833-9eb8-4f0e-a9f0-449e258e5b11","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9rLLaBBNxiJCTsx3oRe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01LXMxquGLs6msQ6VRFG88G2","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/governor/budget.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":11549,"cache_read_input_tokens":19709,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":11549,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9rLKAM4ba4LdZqon5DV","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"793060a9-807d-43d8-ace8-e89ebe2ba547","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:18.203Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"793060a9-807d-43d8-ace8-e89ebe2ba547","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01LXMxquGLs6msQ6VRFG88G2","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Per-agent daily budgets (SIGIL §5) — action and interrupt caps, enforced FAIL-CLOSED (at cap →\n2\tdeny). The spine IS the ledger: spend is derived by counting an agent's own records for the UTC day,\n3\tso there is no separate counter to drift or forge. Caps default to None (uncapped) → budgets are\n4\topt-in and the default dispatch behavior is unchanged; a configured cap is hard-enforced.\n5\t\n6\tToken/cost caps are a documented seam: the mesh does not yet meter provider tokens per action, so\n7\t`daily_actions`/`daily_interrupts` are the enforced dimensions today (interrupt = an A1 event/alert —\n8\tthe SENTINEL failure mode §4.3).\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n12\tfrom typing import Optional, Tuple\n13\t\n14\t\n15\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n16\tclass BudgetCaps:\n17\t    daily_actions: Optional[int] = None       # max records an agent may write per UTC day\n18\t    daily_interrupts: Optional[int] = None    # max A1 events/alerts (the noise budget) per UTC day\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tclass BudgetLedger:\n22\t    def __init__(self, store, caps: Optional[BudgetCaps] = None):\n23\t        self.store = store\n24\t        self.caps = caps or BudgetCaps()\n25\t\n26\t    def spent(self, agent: str, day_iso: str) -> Tuple[int, int]:\n27\t        \"\"\"(#records this agent wrote today, #of those that were interrupt events). `day_iso` is the\n28\t        UTC date prefix (YYYY-MM-DD) — the ts is informational, so bucketing is by wallclock date.\"\"\"\n29\t        actions = interrupts = 0\n30\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n31\t            if r.source != \"agent\" or r.actor != agent:\n32\t                continue\n33\t            if not (r.ts or \"\").startswith(day_iso):\n34\t                continue\n35\t            # count only actions actually TAKEN (auto/queued) — a denial consumes no budget, so a\n36\t            # flood of denied attempts can't self-reinforce the cap.\n37\t            if r.payload.get(\"decision\") not in (\"auto\", \"queued\"):\n38\t                continue\n39\t            actions += 1\n40\t            if r.kind == \"event\":\n41\t                interrupts += 1\n42\t        return actions, interrupts\n43\t\n44\t    def over_budget(self, agent: str, day_iso: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:\n45\t        if self.caps.daily_actions is None and self.caps.daily_interrupts is None:\n46\t            return False, \"\"\n47\t        actions, interrupts = self.spent(agent, day_iso)\n48\t        if self.caps.daily_actions is not None and actions >= self.caps.daily_actions:\n49\t            return True, f\"daily action cap ({self.caps.daily_actions}) reached for {agent}\"\n50\t        if self.caps.daily_interrupts is not None and interrupts >= self.caps.daily_interrupts:\n51\t            return True, f\"daily interrupt cap ({self.caps.daily_interrupts}) reached for {agent}\"\n52\t        return False, \"\"\n53\t"}]},"uuid":"a9d974be-2527-4839-badb-1af41c967947","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:18.230Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"793060a9-807d-43d8-ace8-e89ebe2ba547","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"a9d974be-2527-4839-badb-1af41c967947","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9rLLaBBNxiJCTsx3oRe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Ly9McdpEW5gHHDSJ8J8HPf","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/governor/promotion.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":11549,"cache_read_input_tokens":19709,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":11549,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9rLKAM4ba4LdZqon5DV","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"25d463a8-e228-4a24-974b-8896197ad3e8","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:18.748Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"25d463a8-e228-4a24-974b-8896197ad3e8","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Ly9McdpEW5gHHDSJ8J8HPf","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Per-scope promotion policy (SIGIL §5). An owner may promote a specific (agent, scope) so its A2\n2\tproposals auto-approve instead of queuing — EXCEPT ENVOY, which has NO promotion path (§4.6). A3\n3\tnever promotes.\n4\t\n5\tAUTHENTICATED (Phase 6 red-pen RP-2/RP-4): a grant/revoke is meaningless unless SIGNED by the owner\n6\tkey and verified against the persisted trusted pubkey. `is_promoted` ignores any grant that does not\n7\tverify — so a forged grant written by a prompt-injected agent via `self.store` grants nothing. The\n8\tgate scope is the proposal's RECORD KIND (the real action written), not a self-asserted label\n9\t(RP-3), so a promotion is \"agent X may auto-approve A2 records of kind K.\" \"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom typing import Optional\n13\t\n14\tfrom .authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n15\tfrom .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n16\t\n17\tSIGNAL = \"governor.promotion\"\n18\tNO_PROMOTION_AGENTS = frozenset({\"ENVOY\"})   # structural: outbound stays human-gated forever (§4.6)\n19\t_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\", \"agent\", \"scope\")\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tclass PromotionPolicy:\n23\t    def __init__(self, store, *, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n24\t        self.store = store\n25\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n26\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n27\t\n28\t    def grant(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\"):\n29\t        if agent in NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS:\n30\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\",\n31\t                              payload={\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"decision\": \"refused\", \"agent\": agent,\n32\t                                       \"scope\": scope, \"tier\": \"A0\",\n33\t                                       \"reason\": f\"{agent} has no promotion path (SIGIL §4.6) — outbound stays human-gated\"})\n34\t            return None\n35\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"granted\", \"agent\": agent, \"scope\": scope}\n36\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": \"owner\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n37\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n38\t\n39\t    def revoke(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\") -> int:\n40\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"revoked\", \"agent\": agent, \"scope\": scope}\n41\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": \"owner\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n42\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n43\t\n44\t    def is_promoted(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\") -> bool:\n45\t        if agent in NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS:\n46\t            return False                          # never — structural, not policy\n47\t        state: dict = {}\n48\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n49\t            p = r.payload\n50\t            if p.get(\"signal\") != SIGNAL or p.get(\"state\") not in (\"granted\", \"revoked\"):\n51\t                continue\n52\t            if not verify_signed(p, _CORE, self.trusted_pubkey):\n53\t                continue                          # fail-closed: an unsigned/forged grant grants nothing\n54\t            state[(p.get(\"agent\"), p.get(\"scope\"))] = p[\"state\"]   # latest verified wins\n55\t        return state.get((agent, scope)) == \"granted\" or state.get((agent, \"*\")) == \"granted\"\n56\t"}]},"uuid":"14d5f74e-7020-4aae-bc4a-0281f4fb2228","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:18.762Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"25d463a8-e228-4a24-974b-8896197ad3e8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"2bfcada2-538b-4e75-96db-16c251660ff6","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Cz6pbAeUEXzfBQQhDkfuQu","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Owner identity for governance authentication (Phase 6). The trust anchor is the SAME solo-owner\n2\tEd25519 keypair the spine checkpoint already persists (`KEYS_DIR/owner.priv|pub`, the 1-of-1 trust\n3\troot) — governance events (promotion grants, kill release, approvals) are signed by it and verified\n4\tagainst it. Reads are fail-closed: a missing key returns None, so nothing forged is ever trusted.\n5\t\n6\t`owner_pubkey()`/`owner_keypair()` READ the anchor (never generate — verification must not mint\n7\ttrust). `ensure_owner_keypair()` is for owner-initiated SIGNING actions (CLI engage/grant/approve):\n8\tit generates+persists the anchor once if absent, mirroring the checkpoint's `_owner_keys()`.\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\timport os\n12\tfrom typing import Optional\n13\t\n14\tfrom ..config import KEYS_DIR\n15\tfrom ..reuse import KeyPair, generate_keypair\n16\t\n17\t_PRIV = KEYS_DIR / \"owner.priv\"\n18\t_PUB = KEYS_DIR / \"owner.pub\"\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef owner_pubkey() -> Optional[str]:\n22\t    try:\n23\t        return (_PUB.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip() or None)\n24\t    except OSError:\n25\t        return None\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef owner_keypair() -> Optional[KeyPair]:\n29\t    try:\n30\t        priv = _PRIV.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip()\n31\t        pub = _PUB.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip()\n32\t    except OSError:\n33\t        return None\n34\t    return KeyPair(public_key_b64=pub, private_key_b64=priv) if priv and pub else None\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tdef ensure_owner_keypair() -> KeyPair:\n38\t    \"\"\"Return the persisted owner keypair, generating+persisting it once if absent (owner signing\n39\t    path only). Same key material the checkpoint uses, so governance and the spine head share one\n40\t    owner identity.\"\"\"\n41\t    kp = owner_keypair()\n42\t    if kp is not None:\n43\t        return kp\n44\t    KEYS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n45\t    kp = generate_keypair()\n46\t    _PRIV.write_text(kp.private_key_b64, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n47\t    _PUB.write_text(kp.public_key_b64, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n48\t    for f in (_PRIV,):\n49\t        try:\n50\t            os.chmod(f, 0o600)\n51\t        except OSError:\n52\t            pass\n53\t    return kp\n54\t"}]},"uuid":"db600ac9-97d4-4e3b-b498-4a4d7745b629","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:19.163Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2bfcada2-538b-4e75-96db-16c251660ff6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
