{"parentUuid":"f09b484d-723a-43f3-8055-dc6480887572","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011RgqofdkQHCiQMbShwcuxp","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Shared authentication for governance events (Phase 6). Every state-changing governance event\n2\t(promotion grant/revoke, kill engage/release, approval approve/deny) carries an Ed25519 signature by\n3\tthe OWNER key over its canonical authenticated CORE fields, plus the signer's pubkey. Verification is\n4\tfail-closed: no trusted key, no signature, a pubkey that isn't the trusted owner key, or a tampered\n5\tcore → NOT authentic. This is the primitive that makes \"zero unauthorized A2/A3\" provable from the\n6\tlog rather than promised (a forged governance event never verifies, so it is ignored).\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\tfrom typing import Optional, Sequence\n10\t\n11\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sign, verify_one\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tdef _canon(core: dict) -> bytes:\n15\t    m = canonical_json(core)\n16\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tdef signed_payload(core: dict, owner_key) -> dict:\n20\t    \"\"\"core = the authenticated fields (must be JSON-canonicalizable). Returns core + {sig, pubkey}.\n21\t    With no owner key, sig/pubkey are None → the event will never verify (a caller that requires\n22\t    authentication must reject it).\"\"\"\n23\t    sig = sign(owner_key.private_key_b64, _canon(core)) if owner_key else None\n24\t    return {**core, \"sig\": sig, \"pubkey\": owner_key.public_key_b64 if owner_key else None}\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef verify_signed(payload: dict, core_fields: Sequence[str], trusted_pubkey: Optional[str]) -> bool:\n28\t    \"\"\"True iff `payload` carries a valid owner signature over its declared core fields. Fail-closed.\"\"\"\n29\t    if not trusted_pubkey:\n30\t        return False\n31\t    sig = payload.get(\"sig\")\n32\t    if not sig or payload.get(\"pubkey\") != trusted_pubkey:\n33\t        return False\n34\t    core = {k: payload.get(k) for k in core_fields}\n35\t    return verify_one(trusted_pubkey, _canon(core), sig)\n36\t"}]},"uuid":"103b3590-5aaa-4718-ba97-ef7e80d09129","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:19.798Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f09b484d-723a-43f3-8055-dc6480887572","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"f144e5f4-9bb7-4368-b434-f9674ce37445","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EFCzjQeU9pwkPcNzV1L9BR","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL governor (Phase 6, SIGIL §5 mechanics) — the runtime enforcement of the WARDEN autonomy\n2\tmodel for the AGENT MESH (the Rust `kernel/` WARDEN governs kernel-level actions; this is its\n3\tPython-side mirror for agents). Every agent Proposal is decided here BEFORE it is written:\n4\t\n5\t  • KILL SWITCH (sealed latch): when engaged, the mesh is halted — only A0 observe/read survives.\n6\t  • BUDGETS: per-agent daily action/interrupt caps, fail-closed (over budget → deny + log).\n7\t  • PROMOTION: a per-(agent, scope) grant may auto-approve A2 — EXCEPT ENVOY outbound, which has NO\n8\t    promotion path by construction (SIGIL §4.6). A3 never auto-promotes.\n9\t\n10\tAll state lives on the append-only spine (kill/promotion are events; budget spend is derived from\n11\tthe log), so the governor's every decision is itself auditable — which is what makes the acceptance\n12\tbar (\"zero unauthorized A2/A3 in the audit log\") provable rather than promised. Offense-free by\n13\tdoctrine (assert_no_offense).\"\"\"\n14\tfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n15\t\n16\tassert_no_offense()\n17\t\n18\tfrom .budget import BudgetCaps, BudgetLedger  # noqa: E402\n19\tfrom .core import Decision, Governor, Outcome  # noqa: E402\n20\tfrom .killswitch import KillSwitch  # noqa: E402\n21\tfrom .promotion import PromotionPolicy  # noqa: E402\n22\t\n23\t__all__ = [\"Governor\", \"Decision\", \"Outcome\", \"KillSwitch\", \"BudgetCaps\", \"BudgetLedger\",\n24\t           \"PromotionPolicy\"]\n25\t"}]},"uuid":"c47f40fe-99a4-43b6-8ec1-40280c417f00","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:19.972Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f144e5f4-9bb7-4368-b434-f9674ce37445","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"3e49a29a-6d26-400d-8e31-61d170dc2c0b","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VvENdJKiHRkt1Yetn3Z5wT","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! WARDEN autonomy tiers (SIGIL §5). Every tool invocation is classified A0–A3; the gate\n2\t//! decision follows from the tier. FAIL-CLOSED and TOKEN-BASED: the tool name is split into\n3\t//! whole tokens (on `.`/`_`/`-`/`/`/space) and matched against whole-token sets — never a raw\n4\t//! substring, so \"overwrite\" is not \"write\" and \"forget\" is not \"get\". Danger is checked FIRST,\n5\t//! and A0 (auto, un-queued) is reachable ONLY via a positive safe-verb allowlist; anything not\n6\t//! positively classified — including any unknown or dangerous-target tool — is A3.\n7\t\n8\tuse std::fmt;\n9\t\n10\tuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n11\t\n12\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n13\tpub enum Tier {\n14\t    /// Observe / answer — memory queries, screen read, voice reply. Auto.\n15\t    A0,\n16\t    /// Reversible internal act — write a report/brief, draft-branch, raise an alert. Auto + logged.\n17\t    A1,\n18\t    /// External-visible / semi-reversible — send email, calendar write, purchase < cap. Queued.\n19\t    A2,\n20\t    /// Destructive / financial / security — push protected branch, deploy, delete, spend > cap. Explicit.\n21\t    A3,\n22\t}\n23\t\n24\timpl Tier {\n25\t    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {\n26\t        match self {\n27\t            Tier::A0 => \"A0\",\n28\t            Tier::A1 => \"A1\",\n29\t            Tier::A2 => \"A2\",\n30\t            Tier::A3 => \"A3\",\n31\t        }\n32\t    }\n33\t}\n34\t\n35\timpl fmt::Display for Tier {\n36\t    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n37\t        f.write_str(self.as_str())\n38\t    }\n39\t}\n40\t\n41\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n42\tpub enum Decision {\n43\t    Auto,\n44\t    Queued,\n45\t    ExplicitRequired,\n46\t}\n47\t\n48\timpl Decision {\n49\t    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {\n50\t        match self {\n51\t            Decision::Auto => \"auto\",\n52\t            Decision::Queued => \"queued\",\n53\t            Decision::ExplicitRequired => \"explicit-required\",\n54\t        }\n55\t    }\n56\t    pub fn may_run(&self) -> bool {\n57\t        matches!(self, Decision::Auto)\n58\t    }\n59\t}\n60\t\n61\t/// A3 — destructive verbs, financial ops, crypto/restore ops, and DANGEROUS TARGETS\n62\t/// (secret/identity/network/prod material is A3 regardless of the verb: `secrets.read`,\n63\t/// `iam.policy.write`, `budget.get`, `vault.read`).\n64\tconst A3_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n65\t    // destructive verbs\n66\t    \"push\", \"deploy\", \"delete\", \"destroy\", \"drop\", \"remove\", \"purge\", \"wipe\", \"truncate\",\n67\t    \"overwrite\", \"erase\", \"format\", \"kill\", \"shutdown\", \"reboot\", \"reset\", \"disable\", \"enable\",\n68\t    \"override\", \"force\", \"sudo\", \"exec\", \"eval\", \"chmod\", \"chown\", \"patch\", \"install\", \"uninstall\",\n69\t    // crypto + restore/rollback (a restore silently reverts current state)\n70\t    \"encrypt\", \"decrypt\", \"restore\", \"revert\", \"rollback\", \"recover\",\n71\t    // financial\n72\t    \"spend\", \"purchase\", \"pay\", \"payment\", \"transaction\", \"transfer\", \"refund\", \"invoice\",\n73\t    \"allocate\", \"budget\", \"release\", \"sign\",\n74\t    // security / identity / infra / secret-store TARGETS (dangerous no matter the verb)\n75\t    \"secret\", \"secrets\", \"credential\", \"credentials\", \"token\", \"tokens\", \"key\", \"keys\", \"iam\",\n76\t    \"policy\", \"firewall\", \"acl\", \"role\", \"grant\", \"revoke\", \"rotate\", \"escalate\", \"infra\",\n77\t    \"prod\", \"production\", \"env\", \"master\", \"root\", \"admin\",\n78\t    \"vault\", \"keyring\", \"keychain\", \"keystore\", \"hsm\",\n79\t];\n80\t\n81\t/// A2 — external-visible / semi-reversible: communication, publishing, and bulk DATA EGRESS\n82\t/// (export/dump of a private store must be human-seen, never auto).\n83\tconst A2_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n84\t    \"send\", \"email\", \"smtp\", \"publish\", \"post\", \"message\", \"outbound\", \"webhook\", \"sms\", \"notify\",\n85\t    \"share\", \"upload\", \"invite\", \"calendar\", \"tweet\", \"dm\", \"export\", \"dump\", \"download\", \"sync\",\n86\t];\n87\t\n88\t/// A1 — reversible internal writes (drafts, notes, memory writes, non-push commits). NOTE:\n89\t/// \"snapshot\" is intentionally NOT here — a snapshot RESTORE is destructive (caught by A3\n90\t/// \"restore\"), and snapshot-create alone falls to fail-closed A3 rather than risk auto-run.\n91\tconst A1_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n92\t    \"write\", \"note\", \"brief\", \"report\", \"draft\", \"alert\", \"consolidate\", \"commit\", \"branch\",\n93\t    \"annotate\", \"tag\", \"label\",\n94\t];\n95\t\n96\t/// A0 — POSITIVE allowlist of known-safe observe/answer VERBS only (NO target nouns — a target\n97\t/// noun here would let e.g. `memory.encrypt` auto-run). A tool reaches A0 only by a read-verb\n98\t/// here, or an exact name in `A0_TOOLS`; absence of danger alone is NOT sufficient → fail-closed A3.\n99\tconst A0_VERBS: &[&str] = &[\n100\t    \"read\", \"search\", \"query\", \"get\", \"list\", \"status\", \"recall\", \"observe\", \"answer\",\n101\t    \"view\", \"show\", \"find\", \"frame\", \"peek\", \"describe\", \"inspect\", \"lookup\",\n102\t];\n103\t\n104\t/// A0 — exact-name allowlist for the known read-only memory tools whose names are not verb-clean\n105\t/// (`graph.entity`, `episodic.range`, `threads.open`, `commitments.due`, `contradictions.pending`,\n106\t/// `ingest.status`). Matched on the whole lowercased tool name.\n107\tconst A0_TOOLS: &[&str] = &[\n108\t    \"memory.search\", \"graph.query\", \"graph.entity\", \"episodic.range\", \"ingest.status\",\n109\t    \"threads.open\", \"commitments.due\", \"contradictions.pending\",\n110\t];\n111\t\n112\t/// Split a tool name into whole tokens on `.`/`_`/`-`/`/` and whitespace, lowercased.\n113\tfn tokens(tool: &str) -> Vec<String> {\n114\t    tool.split(|c: char| c == '.' || c == '_' || c == '-' || c == '/' || c.is_whitespace())\n115\t        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())\n116\t        .map(|s| s.to_ascii_lowercase())\n117\t        .collect()\n118\t}\n119\t\n120\t/// Classify a tool name into a tier. Danger FIRST, then A2, A1; A0 only via a positive safe-verb\n121\t/// or an exact safe-tool name; everything else (unknown / not-positively-safe / empty) → A3.\n122\tpub fn classify(tool: &str) -> Tier {\n123\t    let full = tool.to_ascii_lowercase();\n124\t    let tk = tokens(tool);\n125\t    if tk.is_empty() {\n126\t        return Tier::A3;\n127\t    }\n128\t    let has = |set: &[&str]| tk.iter().any(|t| set.contains(&t.as_str()));\n129\t    if has(A3_TOKENS) {\n130\t        return Tier::A3;\n131\t    }\n132\t    if has(A2_TOKENS) {\n133\t        return Tier::A2;\n134\t    }\n135\t    if has(A1_TOKENS) {\n136\t        return Tier::A1;\n137\t    }\n138\t    if A0_TOOLS.contains(&full.as_str()) || has(A0_VERBS) {\n139\t        return Tier::A0;\n140\t    }\n141\t    Tier::A3 // not positively classified → fail-closed to the most-gated tier\n142\t}\n143\t\n144\tpub fn gate(tier: Tier) -> Decision {\n145\t    match tier {\n146\t        Tier::A0 | Tier::A1 => Decision::Auto,\n147\t        Tier::A2 => Decision::Queued,\n148\t        Tier::A3 => Decision::ExplicitRequired,\n149\t    }\n150\t}\n151\t\n152\t#[cfg(test)]\n153\tmod tests {\n154\t    use super::*;\n155\t\n156\t    #[test]\n157\t    fn classification_matches_spec_examples() {\n158\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.search\"), Tier::A0);\n159\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"graph.query\"), Tier::A0);\n160\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.write\"), Tier::A1);\n161\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"brief.compose\"), Tier::A1);\n162\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"email.send\"), Tier::A2);\n163\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"calendar.write\"), Tier::A2); // \"calendar\" (A2) beats \"write\" (A1)\n164\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"git.push\"), Tier::A3);\n165\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"deploy.prod\"), Tier::A3);\n166\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"data.delete\"), Tier::A3);\n167\t    }\n168\t\n169\t    #[test]\n170\t    fn unknown_and_empty_are_fail_closed_a3() {\n171\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"something.unclassified\"), Tier::A3);\n172\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"\"), Tier::A3);\n173\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"weird\"), Tier::A3);\n174\t        assert_eq!(gate(classify(\"something.unclassified\")), Decision::ExplicitRequired);\n175\t    }\n176\t\n177\t    #[test]\n178\t    fn substring_collisions_do_not_lower_the_tier() {\n179\t        // the exact review findings: a dangerous op must NOT ride a benign substring down a tier\n180\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"config.overwrite\"), Tier::A3, \"overwrite != write\");\n181\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"db.overwrite\"), Tier::A3);\n182\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"files.forget\"), Tier::A3, \"forget != get; fail-closed\");\n183\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"budget.override\"), Tier::A3, \"financial + override\");\n184\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"budget.get\"), Tier::A3, \"financial target is A3 regardless of verb\");\n185\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"iam.policy.write\"), Tier::A3, \"identity write is A3, not A1\");\n186\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"firewall.rule.write\"), Tier::A3);\n187\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"transaction.sign\"), Tier::A3, \"financial signing is A3, not A1\");\n188\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"secrets.read\"), Tier::A3, \"secret read is A3, not A0\");\n189\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"credentials.get\"), Tier::A3);\n190\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"account.disable\"), Tier::A3);\n191\t    }\n192\t\n193\t    #[test]\n194\t    fn dangerous_ops_on_safe_targets_do_not_auto_run() {\n195\t        // the CRITICAL re-check finding: a target noun must NOT grant A0 to a dangerous verb\n196\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.encrypt\"), Tier::A3, \"ransomware-shaped op must NOT be A0\");\n197\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"graph.destroy\"), Tier::A3);\n198\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"entity.merge\"), Tier::A3, \"irreversible rewrite → fail-closed, not A0\");\n199\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.export\"), Tier::A2, \"full-store egress must be queued, not auto\");\n200\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.dump\"), Tier::A2);\n201\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"snapshot.restore\"), Tier::A3, \"a restore reverts state → A3\");\n202\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"vault.read\"), Tier::A3, \"secret-store read is A3 regardless of verb\");\n203\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"keyring.get\"), Tier::A3);\n204\t    }\n205\t\n206\t    #[test]\n207\t    fn safe_memory_tools_still_reach_a0() {\n208\t        for t in [\n209\t            \"memory.search\", \"graph.query\", \"graph.entity\", \"episodic.range\", \"ingest.status\",\n210\t {"parentUuid":"ee8c47aa-599c-464f-a390-4fd8e9f26caf","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01LssLLw6wrp8LiZZeLTVo3R","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! WARDEN — the authorization kernel (SIGIL §5). The single choke point every tool invocation\n2\t//! crosses: classify the tool into a tier, derive the fail-closed gate decision, and (after the\n3\t//! caller executes-or-blocks) append a signed, chained action-log record. WARDEN never executes\n4\t//! tools itself; it decides and it records.\n5\t\n6\tuse std::io;\n7\tuse std::path::Path;\n8\t\n9\tuse crate::actionlog::{ActionLog, ActionRecord};\n10\tuse crate::crypto::WardenKey;\n11\tuse crate::registry::Registry;\n12\tuse crate::tiers::{gate, Decision, Tier};\n13\t\n14\tpub struct Warden {\n15\t    log: ActionLog,\n16\t    key: WardenKey,\n17\t    registry: Registry,\n18\t}\n19\t\n20\tpub struct Verdict {\n21\t    pub tier: Tier,\n22\t    pub decision: Decision,\n23\t}\n24\t\n25\timpl Warden {\n26\t    /// Open (or initialize) WARDEN under `dir`: the signing key lives in `dir/keys`, the action\n27\t    /// log at `dir/actionlog.jsonl`.\n28\t    pub fn open(dir: &Path) -> io::Result<Self> {\n29\t        let key = WardenKey::load_or_create(&dir.join(\"keys\"))?;\n30\t        let log = ActionLog::new(dir.join(\"actionlog.jsonl\"));\n31\t        let registry = Registry::load(&dir.join(\"tools.json\"));\n32\t        Ok(Warden { log, key, registry })\n33\t    }\n34\t\n35\t    /// Classify + derive the gate decision (pure — no logging, no side effects). The tier is the\n36\t    /// raise-only registry result (an explicit pin can only make a tool MORE gated than inferred).\n37\t    pub fn decide(&self, tool: &str) -> Verdict {\n38\t        let tier = self.registry.tier(tool);\n39\t        Verdict { tier, decision: gate(tier) }\n40\t    }\n41\t\n42\t    /// Append a signed action-log record for a completed (or blocked) invocation.\n43\t    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]\n44\t    pub fn record(\n45\t        &self,\n46\t        agent: &str,\n47\t        tool: &str,\n48\t        args_hash: &str,\n49\t        verdict: &Verdict,\n50\t        approver: &str,\n51\t        result_hash: &str,\n52\t        ts_epoch: u64,\n53\t    ) -> io::Result<ActionRecord> {\n54\t        self.log.append(\n55\t            &self.key, agent, tool, args_hash,\n56\t            verdict.tier.as_str(), verdict.decision.as_str(),\n57\t            approver, result_hash, ts_epoch,\n58\t        )\n59\t    }\n60\t\n61\t    pub fn verify(&self) -> Result<usize, String> {\n62\t        self.log.verify(&self.key.public_hex())\n63\t    }\n64\t\n65\t    pub fn records(&self) -> io::Result<Vec<ActionRecord>> {\n66\t        self.log.records()\n67\t    }\n68\t\n69\t    /// The on-disk signed head as (count, head_hash) — for the anti-rollback anchor.\n70\t    pub fn head(&self) -> io::Result<Option<(u64, String)>> {\n71\t        self.log.head()\n72\t    }\n73\t\n74\t    pub fn public_hex(&self) -> String {\n75\t        self.key.public_hex()\n76\t    }\n77\t\n78\t    /// Sign a message with the WARDEN key (hex) — used to authenticate anchor-set to the spine.\n79\t    pub fn sign_hex(&self, msg: &[u8]) -> String {\n80\t        self.key.sign_hex(msg)\n81\t    }\n82\t}\n83\t\n84\t#[cfg(test)]\n85\tmod tests {\n86\t    use super::*;\n87\t    use crate::crypto::sha256_hex;\n88\t\n89\t    fn tmp_dir() -> std::path::PathBuf {\n90\t        let mut p = std::env::temp_dir();\n91\t        p.push(format!(\"sigil-warden-{}\", sha256_hex(format!(\"{:?}\", std::thread::current().id()).as_bytes())));\n92\t        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&p);\n93\t        p\n94\t    }\n95\t\n96\t    #[test]\n97\t    fn a0_a1_auto_run_a2_a3_blocked() {\n98\t        let w = Warden::open(&tmp_dir()).unwrap();\n99\t        assert!(w.decide(\"memory.search\").decision.may_run(), \"A0 must auto-run\");\n100\t        assert!(w.decide(\"memory.write\").decision.may_run(), \"A1 must auto-run\");\n101\t        assert!(!w.decide(\"email.send\").decision.may_run(), \"A2 must NOT auto-run\");\n102\t        assert!(!w.decide(\"git.push\").decision.may_run(), \"A3 must NOT auto-run\");\n103\t        assert!(!w.decide(\"unknown.thing\").decision.may_run(), \"unknown must fail closed\");\n104\t    }\n105\t\n106\t    #[test]\n107\t    fn recorded_actions_verify_and_survive_reopen() {\n108\t        let dir = tmp_dir();\n109\t        {\n110\t            let w = Warden::open(&dir).unwrap();\n111\t            let v = w.decide(\"memory.search\");\n112\t            w.record(\"KERNEL\", \"memory.search\", \"b3:aa\", &v, \"auto\", \"b3:res\", 1000).unwrap();\n113\t        }\n114\t        // reopen (loads the same persisted key) and append + verify\n115\t        let w2 = Warden::open(&dir).unwrap();\n116\t        let v = w2.decide(\"email.send\");\n117\t        w2.record(\"ENVOY\", \"email.send\", \"b3:bb\", &v, \"none\", \"blocked:queued\", 1001).unwrap();\n118\t        assert_eq!(w2.verify().unwrap(), 2, \"the whole log must verify under the persisted key\");\n119\t    }\n120\t}\n121\t"}]},"uuid":"812b8ada-cf21-40dc-a08b-f3547f97d0df","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:40.839Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ee8c47aa-599c-464f-a390-4fd8e9f26caf","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"6306c620-7da4-4b8a-966f-b60ac04f412f","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9rNz9C1MtLWBRxdW4QQ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EgPwCiGKtCgc1RH1fD3LaN","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/kernel/src/registry.rs"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":9875,"cache_read_input_tokens":40015,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":9875,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9rNxxVd4Kx1D2K2tWdu","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"ed357f1a-e5ac-4d39-a891-492f3cc9af5e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:56.795Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"ed357f1a-e5ac-4d39-a891-492f3cc9af5e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EgPwCiGKtCgc1RH1fD3LaN","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! Per-tool tier registry (Phase 1.1) — the auditable complement to name-inference. A pin makes\n2\t//! classification EXPLICIT for a named tool. Crucially it is **RAISE-ONLY**: the effective tier\n3\t//! is `max(pin, inferred)`, so the override file (`~/.sigil/warden/tools.json`, plain text a\n4\t//! file-write attacker could edit) can only ever make a tool MORE gated, never less — it can\n5\t//! never downgrade `git.push` to auto. Legitimate de-escalation is intentionally NOT available\n6\t//! via an unsigned file (that would need a signed mechanism; a future addition).\n7\t\n8\tuse std::collections::HashMap;\n9\tuse std::path::Path;\n10\t\n11\tuse crate::tiers::{classify, Tier};\n12\t\n13\tpub struct Registry {\n14\t    pins: HashMap<String, Tier>,\n15\t}\n16\t\n17\tfn parse_tier(s: &str) -> Option<Tier> {\n18\t    match s.trim().to_ascii_uppercase().as_str() {\n19\t        \"A0\" => Some(Tier::A0),\n20\t        \"A1\" => Some(Tier::A1),\n21\t        \"A2\" => Some(Tier::A2),\n22\t        \"A3\" => Some(Tier::A3),\n23\t        _ => None,\n24\t    }\n25\t}\n26\t\n27\timpl Registry {\n28\t    pub fn empty() -> Self {\n29\t        Registry { pins: HashMap::new() }\n30\t    }\n31\t\n32\t    /// Load raise-only pins from a JSON object `{ \"tool.name\": \"A2\", ... }`. Unreadable/absent\n33\t    /// file or malformed entries are ignored (the inference engine still governs). Never fails.\n34\t    pub fn load(path: &Path) -> Self {\n35\t        let mut r = Registry::empty();\n36\t        if let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(path) {\n37\t            if let Ok(map) = serde_json::from_str::<HashMap<String, String>>(&text) {\n38\t                for (tool, tier_s) in map {\n39\t                    if let Some(t) = parse_tier(&tier_s) {\n40\t                        // case-colliding keys resolve to the HIGHER tier (deterministic +\n41\t                        // raise-only-consistent), never last-writer-wins by hash order.\n42\t                        let key = tool.to_ascii_lowercase();\n43\t                        let eff = r.pins.get(&key).map_or(t, |&e| e.max(t));\n44\t                        r.pins.insert(key, eff);\n45\t                    }\n46\t                }\n47\t            }\n48\t        }\n49\t        r\n50\t    }\n51\t\n52\t    /// The effective tier: `max(pin, inferred)`. A pin can only RAISE (never lower) the\n53\t    /// token-inference result, so the override file is not a downgrade attack surface.\n54\t    pub fn tier(&self, tool: &str) -> Tier {\n55\t        let inferred = classify(tool);\n56\t        match self.pins.get(&tool.to_ascii_lowercase()) {\n57\t            Some(&pinned) => pinned.max(inferred),\n58\t            None => inferred,\n59\t        }\n60\t    }\n61\t}\n62\t\n63\t#[cfg(test)]\n64\tmod tests {\n65\t    use super::*;\n66\t\n67\t    #[test]\n68\t    fn a_pin_can_raise_but_never_lower() {\n69\t        let mut r = Registry::empty();\n70\t        r.pins.insert(\"custom.report\".into(), Tier::A3); // owner marks a report tool extra-sensitive\n71\t        assert_eq!(r.tier(\"custom.report\"), Tier::A3, \"pin raises A1→A3\");\n72\t\n73\t        // an ATTACKER pinning a dangerous tool to A0 must NOT downgrade it\n74\t        r.pins.insert(\"git.push\".into(), Tier::A0);\n75\t        assert_eq!(r.tier(\"git.push\"), Tier::A3, \"raise-only: a pin can never lower git.push below its inferred A3\");\n76\t\n77\t        // a pin at/below the inferred tier is a no-op\n78\t        r.pins.insert(\"memory.search\".into(), Tier::A0);\n79\t        assert_eq!(r.tier(\"memory.search\"), Tier::A0);\n80\t    }\n81\t\n82\t    #[test]\n83\t    fn case_colliding_pins_resolve_to_the_higher_tier_deterministically() {\n84\t        let mut p = std::env::temp_dir();\n85\t        p.push(format!(\"sigil-reg-{}.json\", std::process::id()));\n86\t        // both keys lowercase to \"note.write\"; last-writer-wins would be nondeterministic\n87\t        std::fs::write(&p, r#\"{\"Note.Write\":\"A2\",\"note.write\":\"A1\"}\"#).unwrap();\n88\t        let r = Registry::load(&p);\n89\t        assert_eq!(r.tier(\"note.write\"), Tier::A2, \"collision → max pin (A2), raised from inferred A1\");\n90\t        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&p);\n91\t    }\n92\t\n93\t    #[test]\n94\t    fn unregistered_tools_fall_through_to_inference() {\n95\t        let r = Registry::empty();\n96\t        assert_eq!(r.tier(\"memory.search\"), Tier::A0);\n97\t        assert_eq!(r.tier(\"memory.encrypt\"), Tier::A3);\n98\t        assert_eq!(r.tier(\"totally.unknown\"), Tier::A3); // fail-closed inference\n99\t    }\n100\t}\n101\t"}]},"uuid":"e1e10f33-70f2-4841-8ec2-f1838e307872","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:56.816Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ed357f1a-e5ac-4d39-a891-492f3cc9af5e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"e1e10f33-70f2-4841-8ec2-f1838e307872","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9rNz9C1MtLWBRxdW4QQ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Q99i7QyoQKzEqVjfP28ywK","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/approvals.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":9875,"cache_read_input_tokens":40015,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":9875,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9rNxxVd4Kx1D2K2tWdu","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"587cd9cf-6538-4553-be1b-63590fd4ff27","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:57.368Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"587cd9cf-6538-4553-be1b-63590fd4ff27","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Q99i7QyoQKzEqVjfP28ywK","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Approval queue (SIGIL §5, §9.3 mobile bridge) — the human gate for A2/A3 proposals. An agent\n2\tQUEUES a proposal (governor → QUEUE); the owner APPROVES or DENIES it here. Authentication is an\n3\tEd25519 signature over the canonical (target, decision, approver) by the OWNER key.\n4\t\n5\tRED-PEN HARDENING (Phase 6):\n6\t • The trusted pubkey is the PERSISTED owner identity — NOT the key supplied to the queue, so an\n7\t   attacker cannot self-certify by bringing their own key (RP-APPROVAL-2). approve/deny require the\n8\t   supplied signing key to BE the trusted owner key.\n9\t • `pending()` treats a queued item as resolved ONLY when a superseding approval VERIFIES against the\n10\t   trusted owner key, and keys resolution off the SIGNED target_seq (not the raw supersedes_id) — so\n11\t   an unsigned/forged approval can't silently drop an item (RP-1/RP-APPROVAL-1), and a genuine\n12\t   approval of one item can't be replayed onto another (RP-APPROVAL-4). `verify_approval` is thereby\n13\t   on the enforcement path (RP-APPROVAL-3).\n14\t\n15\tThis records the DECISION; it executes no external effect (SIGIL has no send/deploy path). The\n16\ttransport (Telegram/WhatsApp over WireGuard, §9.3) is a seam over this authenticated core.\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex, sign, verify_one\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tSIGNAL = \"governor.approval\"\n25\t_QUEUED_STATUS = \"awaiting-approval\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef _approval_message(target_seq, decision: str, approver: str) -> bytes:\n29\t    m = canonical_json({\"target\": target_seq, \"decision\": decision, \"approver\": approver})\n30\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef verify_approval(record, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str]) -> bool:\n34\t    \"\"\"True iff the approval carries a valid OWNER signature over its (target, decision, approver).\n35\t    Unsigned, wrong-key, or no-trusted-key → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n36\t    p = record.payload if hasattr(record, \"payload\") else record\n37\t    sig = p.get(\"sig\")\n38\t    if not sig or not trusted_pubkey_b64 or p.get(\"pubkey\") != trusted_pubkey_b64:\n39\t        return False\n40\t    msg = _approval_message(p.get(\"target_seq\"), p.get(\"approval\"), p.get(\"approver\"))\n41\t    return verify_one(trusted_pubkey_b64, msg, sig)\n42\t\n43\t\n44\tdef pending(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None) -> List:\n45\t    \"\"\"Queued proposals with NO valid owner approval yet, oldest first. Resolution requires a\n46\t    VERIFIED approval; an unsigned/forged/wrong-key approval leaves the item pending (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n47\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64 is None:\n48\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n49\t        trusted_pubkey_b64 = owner_pubkey()\n50\t    resolved = set()\n51\t    queued = {}\n52\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n53\t        p = r.payload\n54\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SIGNAL and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey_b64):\n55\t            resolved.add(p.get(\"target_seq\"))       # honor the SIGNED target, not raw supersedes_id\n56\t        if p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == _QUEUED_STATUS:\n57\t            queued[r.seq] = r\n58\t    return [r for seq, r in sorted(queued.items()) if seq not in resolved]\n59\t\n60\t\n61\tclass ApprovalError(Exception):\n62\t    pass\n63\t\n64\t\n65\tclass ApprovalQueue:\n66\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, owner_key=None,\n67\t                 trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None):\n68\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n69\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n70\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n71\t        # PINNED to the persisted owner identity — never defaulted to the supplied key (RP-APPROVAL-2)\n72\t        self.trusted_pubkey_b64 = trusted_pubkey_b64 if trusted_pubkey_b64 is not None else owner_pubkey()\n73\t\n74\t    def _target(self, seq: int):\n75\t        for r in pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n76\t            if r.seq == seq:\n77\t                return r\n78\t        raise ApprovalError(f\"seq {seq} is not a pending approval (already decided, or not queued)\")\n79\t\n80\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, *, approver: str, reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n81\t        target = self._target(seq)\n82\t        # the signing key MUST be the trusted owner key — not merely \"some key present\" (RP-APPROVAL-2).\n83\t        if (self.owner_key is None or self.trusted_pubkey_b64 is None\n84\t                or self.owner_key.public_key_b64 != self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n85\t            raise ApprovalError(\"approval requires the OWNER's signing key (missing or not the trusted key)\")\n86\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n87\t        sig = sign(self.owner_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n88\t        payload = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq, \"target_kind\": target.kind,\n89\t                   \"target_tier\": target.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason,\n90\t                   \"pubkey\": self.trusted_pubkey_b64, \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg),\n91\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n92\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n93\t                                 payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n94\t\n95\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n96\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n97\t\n98\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n99\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n100\t"}]},"uuid":"602632a2-1a42-41d4-9665-2eeb701a8a5b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:57.398Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"587cd9cf-6538-4553-be1b-63590fd4ff27","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"602632a2-1a42-41d4-9665-2eeb701a8a5b","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cd9rNz9C1MtLWBRxdW4QQ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Rqfi7ceMRBBUisU5AiYBjT","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/runner.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":9875,"cache_read_input_tokens":40015,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":9875,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cd9rNxxVd4Kx1D2K2tWdu","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"d5f509d1-2a9c-43fd-bf27-b9520963c649","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:57.834Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"d5f509d1-2a9c-43fd-bf27-b9520963c649","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Rqfi7ceMRBBUisU5AiYBjT","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Orchestrate the agent mesh (SIGIL §4). v1 = ARCHIVIST → SENTINEL → STEWARD → ENVOY, each\n2\tgated by its autonomy ceiling and writing provenance-linked spine records. `morning` produces\n3\tthe acceptance deliverable (the unprompted brief, with SENTINEL alerts folded in).\"\"\"\n4\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n5\t\n6\tfrom typing import Optional\n7\t\n8\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n9\tfrom .envoy import Envoy, FileInbox, InboxSource\n10\tfrom .sentinel import Sentinel, SpineActivityWatcher, SystemHealthWatcher\n11\tfrom .steward import Steward\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tdef _sentinel_scan(store: SpineStore):\n15\t    head = store.next_seq - 1\n16\t    return Sentinel(store).run([\n17\t        SpineActivityWatcher(store, since_seq=max(-1, head - 500)),\n18\t        SystemHealthWatcher(),\n19\t    ])\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tdef _load_bastion(store: SpineStore):\n23\t    \"\"\"Build a BASTION from ~/.sigil/bastion-assets.json (+ optional bastion-cve-feed.json) if\n24\t    present; None otherwise. Keeps the default brief path unchanged when no infra is configured.\"\"\"\n25\t    import json\n26\t    import sys\n27\t\n28\t    from ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n29\t    from .bastion import Asset, Bastion\n30\t    inv_f = SIGIL_HOME / \"bastion-assets.json\"\n31\t    if not inv_f.exists():\n32\t        return None\n33\t    try:\n34\t        raw = json.loads(inv_f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n35\t        items = raw.get(\"assets\", raw) if isinstance(raw, dict) else raw\n36\t    except (OSError, ValueError):\n37\t        return None\n38\t    # Build assets PER ITEM: a single malformed entry (typo'd key, wrong type) is skipped with a loud\n39\t    # stderr signal — never all-or-nothing, which would silently hide a real misconfiguration (RP-3).\n40\t    inv = []\n41\t    for a in items if isinstance(items, list) else []:\n42\t        try:\n43\t            inv.append(Asset(name=a[\"name\"], kind=a[\"kind\"], ref=a[\"ref\"], meta=a.get(\"meta\")))\n44\t        except (KeyError, TypeError) as e:\n45\t            print(f\"  [BASTION] skipping malformed asset entry {a!r}: missing/invalid {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n46\t    if not inv:\n47\t        return None\n48\t    feed = []\n49\t    feed_f = SIGIL_HOME / \"bastion-cve-feed.json\"\n50\t    if feed_f.exists():\n51\t        try:\n52\t            feed = json.loads(feed_f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n53\t        except (OSError, ValueError):\n54\t            feed = []\n55\t    return Bastion(store, inventory=inv, cve_feed=feed)\n56\t\n57\t\n58\tdef morning(store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, date_label: str = \"today\", bastion=None) -> dict:\n59\t    \"\"\"Run SENTINEL + BASTION (so their alerts/findings are fresh), then STEWARD's brief which folds\n60\t    them in. `bastion` may be injected (tests); otherwise it auto-loads from config if present.\"\"\"\n61\t    store = store or SpineStore()\n62\t    sent = _sentinel_scan(store)\n63\t    b = bastion if bastion is not None else _load_bastion(store)\n64\t    bres = b.run() if b is not None else None\n65\t    stew = Steward(store)\n66\t    stew_res = stew.run(date_label=date_label)\n67\t    brief = stew.brief_text(date_label=date_label)   # composed AFTER SENTINEL/BASTION wrote records\n68\t    out = {\"sentinel\": sent, \"steward\": stew_res, \"brief\": brief}\n69\t    if bres is not None:\n70\t        out[\"bastion\"] = bres\n71\t    return out\n72\t\n73\t\n74\tdef triage(store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, inbox: Optional[InboxSource] = None,\n75\t           inbox_path: Optional[str] = None) -> dict:\n76\t    store = store or SpineStore()\n77\t    src = inbox or FileInbox(inbox_path or \"/home/kali/.sigil/inbox.json\")\n78\t    res = Envoy(store).run(src)\n79\t    return {\"envoy\": res}\n80\t\n81\t\n82\tdef run_all(store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, inbox_path: Optional[str] = None,\n83\t            consolidate: bool = False) -> dict:\n84\t    store = store or SpineStore()\n85\t    out: dict = {}\n86\t    if consolidate:\n87\t        from .archivist import Archivist\n88\t        out[\"archivist\"] = Archivist(store).run()\n89\t    out.update(morning(store))\n90\t    out.update(triage(store, inbox_path=inbox_path))\n91\t    return out\n92\t"}]},"uuid":"14fda8b3-90af-495d-ab38-acf3f174f2f7","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:57.862Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d5f509d1-2a9c-43fd-bf27-b9520963c649","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

All paths local-first, owner-only.\"\"\"\n2\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n3\t\n4\timport os\n5\tfrom pathlib import Path\n6\t\n7\t# --- data root (runtime, 0700) --------------------------------------------------------\n8\tSIGIL_HOME = Path(os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_HOME\", str(Path.home() / \".sigil\")))\n9\t\n10\t\n11\tdef _load_env_file() -> None:\n12\t    \"\"\"Load persisted `KEY=VALUE` settings from ~/.sigil/sigil.env so a value set once\n13\t    (e.g. SIGIL_QDRANT_URL for server mode) reaches BOTH the CLI and the MCP server that\n14\t    Claude spawns without an explicit env. The real environment always wins (setdefault).\"\"\"\n15\t    f = SIGIL_HOME / \"sigil.env\"\n16\t    if not f.exists():\n17\t        return\n18\t    for line in f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines():\n19\t        line = line.strip()\n20\t        if not line or line.startswith(\"#\") or \"=\" not in line:\n21\t            continue\n22\t        k, _, v = line.partition(\"=\")\n23\t        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\n24\t\n25\t\n26\t_load_env_file()\n27\t\n28\tSPINE_PATH = SIGIL_HOME / \"spine\" / \"spine.jsonl\"\n29\tHEAD_PATH = SIGIL_HOME / \"spine\" / \"head.json\"\n30\tKEYS_DIR = SIGIL_HOME / \"spine\" / \"keys\"\n31\tCACHE_DIR = SIGIL_HOME / \"cache\"\n32\t\n33\t# --- ingestion sources ----------------------------------------------------------------\n34\tCLAUDE_PROJECTS = Path(os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_CLAUDE_PROJECTS\", str(Path.home() / \".claude\" / \"projects\")))\n35\t# Phase-0a thin slice: PENTEST-main only. Slugified cwd = dir name under ~/.claude/projects.\n36\tPROJECT_ALLOWLIST = os.environ.get(\n37\t    \"SIGIL_PROJECT_ALLOWLIST\", \"-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main\"\n38\t).split(\",\")\n39\t# ephemeral CRUCIBLE-engine runs to filter out\n40\tEPHEMERAL_PREFIXES = (\"-tmp-crucible-cc-\", \"-tmp-\")\n41\t\n42\t# --- vectors (Qdrant, local/embedded — no server, no Docker; in-process, file-backed) -\n43\tQDRANT_PATH = SIGIL_HOME / \"qdrant\"                       # embedded local-mode store\n44\tQDRANT_URL = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_QDRANT_URL\", \"\")       # set to switch to a server later\n45\tQDRANT_COLLECTION = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_QDRANT_COLLECTION\", \"sigil_memory\")\n46\tEMBED_MODEL = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_EMBED_MODEL\", \"BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5\")\n47\tEMBED_DIM = 384\n48\t\n49\t# --- graph (Kùzu, deterministic mirror of the spine) ----------------------------------\n50\tGRAPH_DIR = SIGIL_HOME / \"graph\"                          # base; holds current/ + staging/ (atomic swap)\n51\t\n52\t# --- identity / scope -----------------------------------------------------------------\n53\tSCOPE = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_SCOPE\", \"sigil\")          # the owner's single scope\n54\tOWNER_KEY_ID = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_OWNER_KEY_ID\", \"owner\")\n55\t\n56\t\n57\tdef ensure_dirs() -> None:\n58\t    for d in (SIGIL_HOME, SPINE_PATH.parent, KEYS_DIR, CACHE_DIR):\n59\t        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n60\t        try:\n61\t            os.chmod(d, 0o700)\n62\t        except OSError:\n63\t            pass\n64\t"}]},"uuid":"8b1c9075-1621-46df-a463-120064d9bd80","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:38:58.141Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4553b2ab-52d3-4502-be91-a01bdf4d6a99","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"66c3dc30-33b1-4fc4-a2eb-5f26739edfe6","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QVexC9bgBXbUZ2xBUjJqtN","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! SIGIL KERNEL — the palette (Phase 1). A command interface to the KERNEL: route an intent\n2\t//! through the cognition cascade (T0 → answer/escalate/dispatch), gate every tool invocation\n3\t//! through WARDEN (tier A0–A3, fail-closed), and append a signed, chained action-log record. The\n4\t//! self-audit replays that log verbatim (\"what did you do and why\", C18). A global hotkey +\n5\t//! voice daemon are Phase 1.5/2; this CLI meets the acceptance bar: a full task round-trip,\n6\t//! logged & tiered.\n7\t\n8\tuse std::path::PathBuf;\n9\tuse std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};\n10\t\n11\tuse clap::{Parser, Subcommand};\n12\tuse sigil_kernel::crypto::sha256_hex;\n13\tuse sigil_kernel::router::{route, Route};\n14\tuse sigil_kernel::tiers::Tier;\n15\tuse sigil_kernel::warden::{Verdict, Warden};\n16\tuse sigil_kernel::{anchor, cascade, memory};\n17\t\n18\t#[derive(Parser)]\n19\t#[command(name = \"sigil-kernel\", about = \"SIGIL KERNEL — routed, gated, logged intent\")]\n20\tstruct Cli {\n21\t    #[command(subcommand)]\n22\t    cmd: Cmd,\n23\t}\n24\t\n25\t#[derive(Subcommand)]\n26\tenum Cmd {\n27\t    /// Route an intent through the cascade (T0 classify → dispatch), gated + logged by WARDEN.\n28\t    Ask {\n29\t        #[arg(required = true, trailing_var_arg = true)]\n30\t        intent: Vec<String>,\n31\t    },\n32\t    /// Invoke a named tool directly through WARDEN — demonstrates tier classification + gating.\n33\t    Do {\n34\t        tool: String,\n35\t        #[arg(trailing_var_arg = true)]\n36\t        args: Vec<String>,\n37\t    },\n38\t    /// Replay the signed action log verbatim (self-audit, C18).\n39\t    Audit,\n40\t    /// Verify the action log's chain + signatures + the spine-anchored anti-rollback high-water.\n41\t    /// FAILS CLOSED if the anchor is unreachable/unestablished; pass --allow-unanchored for a\n42\t    /// genuine first run / offline check (weaker: local integrity only).\n43\t    Verify {\n44\t        #[arg(long)]\n45\t        allow_unanchored: bool,\n46\t    },\n47\t    /// Cross-anchor the current action-log head into the append-only Phase-0 spine (anti-rollback).\n48\t    Checkpoint,\n49\t    /// Print the WARDEN public key.\n50\t    Pubkey,\n51\t}\n52\t\n53\tfn warden_dir() -> PathBuf {\n54\t    let home = std::env::var(\"SIGIL_HOME\")\n55\t        .unwrap_or_else(|_| format!(\"{}/.sigil\", std::env::var(\"HOME\").unwrap_or_else(|_| \".\".into())));\n56\t    PathBuf::from(home).join(\"warden\")\n57\t}\n58\t\n59\tfn now() -> u64 {\n60\t    SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).map(|d| d.as_secs()).unwrap_or(0)\n61\t}\n62\t\n63\tfn args_hash(s: &str) -> String {\n64\t    format!(\"b3:{}\", &sha256_hex(s.as_bytes())[..12])\n65\t}\n66\t\n67\tfn main() {\n68\t    let cli = Cli::parse();\n69\t    let w = Warden::open(&warden_dir()).unwrap_or_else(|e| {\n70\t        eprintln!(\"cannot open WARDEN: {e}\");\n71\t        std::process::exit(1);\n72\t    });\n73\t\n74\t    match cli.cmd {\n75\t        Cmd::Ask { intent } => cmd_ask(&w, &intent.join(\" \")),\n76\t        Cmd::Do { tool, args } => cmd_do(&w, &tool, &args.join(\" \")),\n77\t        Cmd::Audit => cmd_audit(&w),\n78\t        Cmd::Verify { allow_unanchored } => cmd_verify(&w, allow_unanchored),\n79\t        Cmd::Checkpoint => cmd_checkpoint(&w),\n80\t        Cmd::Pubkey => println!(\"{}\", w.public_hex()),\n81\t    }\n82\t}\n83\t\n84\tfn anchor_set(w: &Warden, count: u64, head_hash: &str) -> std::io::Result<()> {\n85\t    let pubkey = w.public_hex();\n86\t    let sig = w.sign_hex(anchor::anchor_msg(count, head_hash, &pubkey).as_bytes());\n87\t    anchor::set(count, head_hash, &pubkey, &sig)\n88\t}\n89\t\n90\t/// Anchor the current head into the spine — best-effort, never fatal to the hot path.\n91\tfn anchor_head(w: &Warden) {\n92\t    if let Ok(Some((count, head_hash))) = w.head() {\n93\t        if count > 0 {\n94\t            let _ = anchor_set(w, count, &head_hash);\n95\t        }\n96\t    }\n97\t}\n98\t\n99\tfn cmd_checkpoint(w: &Warden) {\n100\t    match w.head() {\n101\t        Ok(Some((count, head_hash))) => match anchor_set(w, count, &head_hash) {\n102\t            Ok(()) => println!(\"anchored action-log head into the spine: count={count} head={}\", &head_hash[..16.min(head_hash.len())]),\n103\t            Err(e) => eprintln!(\"anchor failed: {e}\"),\n104\t        },\n105\t        Ok(None) => println!(\"nothing to anchor (action log empty)\"),\n106\t        Err(e) => eprintln!(\"cannot read head: {e}\"),\n107\t    }\n108\t}\n109\t\n110\tfn cmd_ask(w: &Warden, intent: &str) {\n111\t    let r = route(intent);\n112\t    // Route → the tool the KERNEL will invoke. Cognition (answer/plan) is A0-class (no external\n113\t    // effect); a real agent dispatch is recorded as an A1 intent note (the mesh is Phase 3).\n114\t    let tool = match r {\n115\t        Route::AnswerLocal => \"memory.search\",\n116\t        Route::EscalateT1 => \"cascade.answer.t1\",\n117\t        Route::EscalateT2 => \"cascade.answer.t2\",\n118\t        Route::DispatchAgent => \"agent.dispatch.note\",\n119\t    };\n120\t    let v = w.decide(tool);\n121\t    println!(\"[T0 {} → {}]  WARDEN: {} {}\", r.as_str(), tool, v.tier, v.decision.as_str());\n122\t\n123\t    if !v.decision.may_run() {\n124\t        block(w, \"KERNEL\", tool, intent, &v);\n125\t        return;\n126\t    }\n127\t\n128\t    let output = match r {\n129\t        Route::AnswerLocal => memory::search(intent, 5).unwrap_or_else(|e| format!(\"(memory unavailable: {e})\")),\n130\t        Route::EscalateT1 => cascade::run(intent, cascade::FAST).unwrap_or_else(|e| format!(\"(T1 unavailable: {e})\")),\n131\t        Route::EscalateT2 => cascade::run(intent, cascade::DEEP).unwrap_or_else(|e| format!(\"(T2 unavailable: {e})\")),\n132\t        Route::DispatchAgent => format!(\n133\t            \"[dispatch] the agent mesh (ARTIFICER/SCHOLAR/ENVOY) is Phase 3; the KERNEL recorded this intent:\\n  {intent}\"\n134\t        ),\n135\t    };\n136\t    finish(w, \"KERNEL\", tool, intent, &v, &output);\n137\t}\n138\t\n139\tfn cmd_do(w: &Warden, tool: &str, args: &str) {\n140\t    let v = w.decide(tool);\n141\t    println!(\"[direct tool {}]  WARDEN: {} {}\", tool, v.tier, v.decision.as_str());\n142\t    if !v.decision.may_run() {\n143\t        block(w, \"KERNEL\", tool, args, &v);\n144\t        return;\n145\t    }\n146\t    // v1 has no real tool executors (those are the Phase-3 agents) — record an executed stub so\n147\t    // the round-trip + tiering + signed log are demonstrable end-to-end.\n148\t    let output = format!(\"[executed] {tool} {args}\").trim_end().to_string();\n149\t    finish(w, \"KERNEL\", tool, args, &v, &output);\n150\t}\n151\t\n152\tfn finish(w: &Warden, agent: &str, tool: &str, args: &str, v: &Verdict, output: &str) {\n153\t    let rh = format!(\"b3:{}\", &sha256_hex(output.as_bytes())[..12]);\n154\t    match w.record(agent, tool, &args_hash(args), v, \"auto\", &rh, now()) {\n155\t        Ok(rec) => {\n156\t            println!(\"\\n{output}\\n\");\n157\t            println!(\n158\t                \"[WARDEN {} {} — logged @ action spine seq {} · {}]\",\n159\t                v.tier, v.decision.as_str(), rec.seq, &rec.entry_hash[..16]\n160\t            );\n161\t        }\n162\t        Err(e) => eprintln!(\"action-log write failed: {e}\"),\n163\t    }\n164\t}\n165\t\n166\tfn block(w: &Warden, agent: &str, tool: &str, args: &str, v: &Verdict) {\n167\t    let need = if v.tier == Tier::A3 { \"explicit per-action approval (no promotion)\" } else { \"one-tap approval\" };\n168\t    let _ = w.record(agent, tool, &args_hash(args), v, \"none\", \"blocked:awaiting-approval\", now());\n169\t    println!(\"[BLOCKED — {} requires {}; not executed. Logged as awaiting-approval.]\", v.tier, need);\n170\t    anchor_head(w); // protect the evidence that a dangerous action was attempted (anti-rollback)\n171\t}\n172\t\n173\tfn cmd_audit(w: &Warden) {\n174\t    match w.records() {\n175\t        Ok(recs) if recs.is_empty() => println!(\"(action log empty — no actions yet)\"),\n176\t        Ok(recs) => {\n177\t            println!(\"SIGIL action spine — {} record(s), verbatim (C18 self-audit):\\n\", recs.len());\n178\t            for r in recs {\n179\t                println!(\n180\t                    \"  seq {:<3} {:<4} {:<20} {:<10} agent={:<10} approver={:<12} ts={}\",\n181\t                    r.seq, r.tier, r.tool, r.decision, r.agent, r.approver, r.ts\n182\t                );\n183\t            }\n184\t        }\n185\t        Err(e) => eprintln!(\"audit failed: {e}\"),\n186\t    }\n187\t}\n188\t\n189\tfn cmd_verify(w: &Warden, allow_unanchored: bool) {\n190\t    let n = match w.verify() {\n191\t        Ok(n) => n,\n192\t        Err(e) => {\n193\t            eprintln!(\"action log FAIL: {e}\");\n194\t            std::process::exit(2);\n195\t        }\n196\t    };\n197\t    let on_disk = w.head().ok().flatten().map(|(c, _)| c).unwrap_or(0);\n198\t\n199\t    // Cross-check the spine-anchored monotonic high-water. The anchor read itself FAILS CLOSED:\n200\t    // a bridge error, a tampered spine (the bridge exits non-zero), OR an established-but-now-zero\n201\t    // high-water are all treated as UNVERIFIABLE — because the checker (bridge + spine) is under\n202\t    // the same file-write attacker the anchor defends against, resolving its failure as \"OK\" would\n203\t    // hand the attacker a clean bill. `--allow-unanchored` is the explicit escape for a genuine\n204\t    // first run / offline check.\n205\t    match anchor::high_water(&w.public_hex()) {\n206\t        Ok((hw, _)) if on_disk < hw => {\n207\t            eprintln!(\"action log FAIL: ROLLBACK — on-disk head count {on_disk} < spine-anchored high-water {hw} (recent records erased)\");\n208\t            std::process::exit(2);\n209\t        }\n210\t        Ok((hw, _)) if hw > 0 => {\n211\t            println!(\"action log OK: {n} record(s) verify — chain + signatures + anti-rollback anchor (spine high-water {hw})\");\n212\t        }\n213\t        // hw == 0 (never anchored for this key) or Err (bridge/spine unavailable) → unverifiable\n214\t        other => {\n215\t            if allow_unanchored {\n216\t                let why = if other.is_ok() { \"no anchor established yet\" } else { \"anchor bridge unavailable\" };\n217\t                println!(\"action log OK (LOCAL ONLY): {n} record(s) verify — chain + signatures; anti-rollback UNVERIFIED ({why}, --allow-unanchored)\");\n218\t            } else if n == 0 {\n219\t                println!(\"action log OK: empty log, nothing to anchor\");\n220\t            } else {\n221\t                eprintln!(\"action log UNVERIFIABLE: {n} record(s) pass local checks but the anti-rollback anchor is unreachable/unestablished — cannot certify freshness (a rollback+bridge-sabotage looks identical). Re-run with --allow-unanchored only if this is a genuine first run/offline.\");\n222\t                std::process::exit(3);\n223\t            }\n224\t        }\n225\t    }\n226\t}\n227\t"}]},"uuid":"624de126-6d53-492e-ac4c-dd4a068b507d","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:39:07.923Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"66c3dc30-33b1-4fc4-a2eb-5f26739edfe6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"3d5a5406-1ab6-4ad0-8e91-7f2e373744db","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019aMbmPRMTuiZhmMraY6G2t","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Voice → KERNEL dispatch. Recognized text is routed through the Phase-1 KERNEL (`sigil-kernel\n2\task`), so a voice request crosses the SAME T0 router + WARDEN gate + signed action log as any\n3\tother — voice is just another interface onto the one authorized path. The spoken response is the\n4\tKERNEL's answer body (the bracketed [T0…]/[WARDEN…] status lines are stripped).\"\"\"\n5\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n6\t\n7\timport subprocess\n8\tfrom pathlib import Path\n9\t\n10\t\n11\tdef _default_bin() -> str:\n12\t    for p in (\"kernel/target/release/sigil-kernel\", \"kernel/target/debug/sigil-kernel\"):\n13\t        f = Path(\"/home/kali/sigil\") / p\n14\t        if f.exists():\n15\t            return str(f)\n16\t    return \"sigil-kernel\"\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tclass KernelDispatch:\n20\t    def __init__(self, kernel_bin: str | None = None, timeout: int = 60):\n21\t        self.kernel_bin = kernel_bin or _default_bin()\n22\t        self.timeout = timeout\n23\t\n24\t    # exact KERNEL status-line prefixes to strip (so a legitimate answer line that merely starts\n25\t    # with '[' is NOT dropped, and a BLOCKED/error is NOT masked as an answer).\n26\t    _STATUS = (\"[t0\", \"[warden\", \"[blocked\", \"[direct\", \"[dispatch\")\n27\t\n28\t    def send(self, text: str) -> str:\n29\t        text = (text or \"\").strip()\n30\t        if not text:\n31\t            return \"I didn't catch that.\"\n32\t        try:\n33\t            proc = subprocess.run([self.kernel_bin, \"ask\", text],\n34\t                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n35\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as e:\n36\t            return f\"the kernel is unavailable ({e})\"\n37\t        if proc.returncode != 0:\n38\t            tail = (proc.stderr or proc.stdout).strip().splitlines()\n39\t            return f\"that request was refused or errored: {tail[-1][:200]}\" if tail else \"that request was refused.\"\n40\t        if any(ln.lstrip().lower().startswith(\"[blocked\") for ln in proc.stdout.splitlines()):\n41\t            return \"that action needs your approval — it wasn't run.\"\n42\t        body = [ln.strip() for ln in proc.stdout.splitlines()\n43\t                if ln.strip() and not any(ln.lstrip().lower().startswith(s) for s in self._STATUS)]\n44\t        answer = \" \".join(body).strip()\n45\t        return answer[:600] if answer else \"there's no answer for that in memory yet.\"\n46\t"}]},"uuid":"becfa5a0-0e9c-4c91-9d1f-a6a8dcf0a65e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:39:08.512Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3d5a5406-1ab6-4ad0-8e91-7f2e373744db","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"462020e7-0c12-472e-b3a0-012754e81ad1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"eb39b3f8-d31e-4f60-9749-66467e0d38c7","agentId":"a89e2c94699dcff37","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017ubXBYKTgMRuYj6yvwATzV","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SCHOLAR (SIGIL §4.5) — research & analysis: long-horizon research, sourced synthesis. Ceiling\n2\tA1 (research never touches external state). Its focus is EPISTEMICS: every claim carries a\n3\tsource and a verbatim quote, confidence is explicit, and a claim that does NOT verify against its\n4\tcited source is DEMOTED (not asserted) — the same serve-the-quote gate that governs the\n5\tconsolidation, reused here. The output is a `report` record grounded in real sources, so future\n6\trecall of the research is cited and honest, not confident. The synthesizer is pluggable\n7\t(`ClaudeSynthesizer` via `claude -p`, or a deterministic double).\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport json\n11\timport re\n12\timport subprocess\n13\tfrom typing import Dict, List, Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n14\t\n15\tfrom ..consolidate.gate import salient   # reuse the veracity tokenizer\n16\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n17\tfrom .sources import read_source\n18\t\n19\t_WS = re.compile(r\"\\s+\")\n20\t_MIN_QUOTE_SALIENT = 2\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tdef _norm(t: str) -> str:\n24\t    return _WS.sub(\" \", (t or \"\")).strip().lower()\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef grounds_in_source(quote: str, source_text: str) -> bool:\n28\t    \"\"\"A claim's quote must be verbatim in the cited source AND specific (≥2 salient tokens) —\n29\t    exactly the serve-the-quote discipline, applied to research sources.\"\"\"\n30\t    q = _norm(quote)\n31\t    if not q or len(salient(quote)) < _MIN_QUOTE_SALIENT:\n32\t        return False\n33\t    return q in _norm(source_text)\n34\t\n35\t\n36\t@runtime_checkable\n37\tclass Synthesizer(Protocol):\n38\t    def synthesize(self, question: str, docs: Dict[str, str]) -> List[dict]: ...  # [{claim,source,quote,confidence}]\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tclass ClaudeSynthesizer:\n42\t    \"\"\"`claude -p` over the sources → JSON claims, each citing a source ref + a verbatim quote.\"\"\"\n43\t    def __init__(self, claude_bin: str = \"/home/kali/.local/bin/claude\",\n44\t                 model: str = \"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001\", timeout: int = 180):\n45\t        self.claude_bin, self.model, self.timeout = claude_bin, model, timeout\n46\t\n47\t    def synthesize(self, question: str, docs: Dict[str, str]) -> List[dict]:\n48\t        rendered = \"\\n\\n\".join(f\"[SOURCE {ref}]\\n{text[:4000]}\" for ref, text in docs.items())\n49\t        prompt = (\n50\t            \"You are a research analyst. Answer the QUESTION using ONLY the SOURCES. For every claim, \"\n51\t            \"cite the exact source ref and copy a VERBATIM quote from it that supports the claim; if a \"\n52\t            \"source does not support a claim, do not make it. If sources DISAGREE, report both. Return \"\n53\t            'ONLY a JSON array: [{\"claim\":\"...\",\"source\":\"<ref>\",\"quote\":\"<verbatim>\",\"confidence\":<0..1>}].'\n54\t            f\"\\n\\nQUESTION: {question}\\n\\nSOURCES:\\n{rendered}\\n\"\n55\t        )\n56\t        try:\n57\t            proc = subprocess.run([self.claude_bin, \"-p\", prompt, \"--model\", self.model],\n58\t                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n59\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n60\t            return []\n61\t        m = re.search(r\"\\[.*\\]\", proc.stdout, re.S)\n62\t        if not m:\n63\t            return []\n64\t        try:\n65\t            items = json.loads(m.group(0))\n66\t        except json.JSONDecodeError:\n67\t            return []\n68\t        out = []\n69\t        for it in items if isinstance(items, list) else []:\n70\t            if isinstance(it, dict) and it.get(\"claim\") and it.get(\"source\"):\n71\t                out.append({\"claim\": str(it[\"claim\"]), \"source\": str(it[\"source\"]),\n72\t                            \"quote\": str(it.get(\"quote\", \"\")), \"confidence\": float(it.get(\"confidence\", 0.5) or 0.5)})\n73\t        return out\n74\t\n75\t"}]},"uuid":"ffd20780-91e2-4629-92bc-4b81f70d2e30","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:39:20.573Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"462020e7-0c12-472e-b3a0-012754e81ad1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

SIGIL\n4\timports NO `framework.*` module at all, which is the strongest form of the sovereignty\n5\tdoctrine (§1.3 / §12): the offensive engine cannot even be loaded into a SIGIL process.\n6\t`assert_no_offense()` proves it.\n7\t\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport sys\n11\t\n12\tfrom .canonical import canonical_json, digest_payload, evidence_signing_bytes, sha256_hex\n13\tfrom .chain import append_entry, build_chain, sign_head, verify_chain, verify_head\n14\tfrom .crypto import generate_keypair, sign, verify_one, verify_threshold, KeyPair\n15\tfrom .models import AuthorizerKey, ChainEntry, Signature, SignedChainHead, TrustRoot\n16\t\n17\t__all__ = [\n18\t    \"canonical_json\", \"digest_payload\", \"evidence_signing_bytes\", \"sha256_hex\",\n19\t    \"append_entry\", \"build_chain\", \"sign_head\", \"verify_chain\", \"verify_head\",\n20\t    \"generate_keypair\", \"sign\", \"verify_one\", \"verify_threshold\", \"KeyPair\",\n21\t    \"AuthorizerKey\", \"ChainEntry\", \"Signature\", \"SignedChainHead\", \"TrustRoot\",\n22\t    \"assert_no_offense\",\n23\t]\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef assert_no_offense() -> None:\n27\t    \"\"\"Fail-closed: NO framework.* module may be loaded in a SIGIL process (doctrine §12).\"\"\"\n28\t    leaked = [m for m in sys.modules if m == \"framework\" or m.startswith(\"framework.\")]\n29\t    if leaked:\n30\t        raise RuntimeError(f\"SIGIL sovereignty violation: engine modules loaded: {leaked}\")\n31\t"}]},"uuid":"586b82ed-61fa-420f-a7dc-1076a65f0e0f","timestamp":"2026-07-18T16:39:45.747Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8479a07b-d94a-43f8-a02e-ab1f1ef106df","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
